### Progress and findings

- 2026-10-25 · **New problem.** The first blind listening on real songs was negative. On five songs the human heard the five best drive candidates against the separated guitar and said 'so bad I can't even compare them'. It contradicts the open listens of 22 October that called the same kind of match 'pretty good'. Heard blind but answered aloud and not scored (evidence class C). Two stopped jobs (a preset-corpus render and a separation re-score) can resume. _(Evidence: [T223](../../board/TASKS.md#t223 "Blind validation of AFx-Rep against separated stems") AFx-Rep stem-validation pack note (unscored); Decision log)_
- 2026-10-25 · **Decision that changed the plan.** The human refocused the product: drive, then delay or reverb, then equaliser, with no amps or cabinets for now; delay was then put on hold, so the chain is drive, equaliser, reverb. An amp may be tested only as a stand-in for the drive. [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") was closed as superseded and the 'yes go' bundle approved the governance changes (recorded as provisional). _(Evidence: Decision log; Gate checklists)_
- 2026-10-25 · **Improvement.** Colour pilots on three then seven songs: a light reverb after the drive was preferred on all seven (open listening), fitted equaliser variants could not be told apart (kept at the human's request), the amp stand-in was mixed, and one blind Mother pack favoured the fitted equaliser. Plate sounded best openly but was not preferred in the later blind ranks. Directional only (class C, one B). _(Evidence: [T228](../../board/TASKS.md#t228 "Colour pilot (human 2026-10-25 \"focus on our distortion model… drive…") colour pilot note; [T229](../../board/TASKS.md#t229 "Colour comparison on seven songs") seven-song colour comparison note; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-25 · **New problem.** The reverb pilot found no signal that agrees with the human: four pre-registered candidate signals all failed (best 0.05 rank correlation), although the listener was very consistent (mean rank difference 0.33 against 2.67 by chance). The human rejected a fixed reverb default and asked for a model. _(Evidence: [T230](../../board/TASKS.md#t230 "Reverb-model pilot (human 2026-10-25 \"yes\")") reverb-model pilot note; [T230](../../board/TASKS.md#t230 "Reverb-model pilot (human 2026-10-25 \"yes\")") pre-registered scoring: listener repeat consistency; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-25 · **Improvement.** A blind reverb estimator was built and validated on 189 known reverbs (decay ordering 36 of 40, mix always monotone, type not separable). On real stems it reads a tail on only 2 of 7; the rest is separator leftovers. The human said not to work on the separator. _(Evidence: [T231](../../board/TASKS.md#t231 "Blind reverb estimator") blind reverb estimator note; [T231](../../board/TASKS.md#t231 "Blind reverb estimator") reverb estimator validation rows; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-25 · **New problem.** The pre-registered confirmation of the estimator held on Mother (named closest ranked 1 of 7, furthest 7 of 7) and was not reproducible on Territorial; pooled it is not confirmed. One song is not a result. _(Evidence: [T232](../../board/TASKS.md#t232 "Pre-registered confirmation of the T231 reverb estimator on Mother…") reverb estimator confirmation note; [T232](../../board/TASKS.md#t232 "Pre-registered confirmation of the T231 reverb estimator on Mother…") pre-registered confirmation scoring)_
- 2026-10-25 · **Milestone.** [Gate 3](../../board/GATES.md#gate-3--blind-prediction-of-a-single-block-without-the-dry-track-new-human-2026-10-18 "Gate 3 — blind prediction of a single block without the dry track (new, human 2026-10-18)") advanced from 0 to 1 of 4 criteria: a sealed 120-item blind set was generated on the reserved third guitarist's recordings, with bars written down first. The bars are proposed by benchmark and not approved by the human; nothing is scored. _(Evidence: [Gate 3](../../board/GATES.md#gate-3--blind-prediction-of-a-single-block-without-the-dry-track-new-human-2026-10-18 "Gate 3 — blind prediction of a single block without the dry track (new, human 2026-10-18)") sealed set gate3-v1 note; [Gate 3](../../board/GATES.md#gate-3--blind-prediction-of-a-single-block-without-the-dry-track-new-human-2026-10-18 "Gate 3 — blind prediction of a single block without the dry track (new, human 2026-10-18)") set sealed: 120 items; [Gate 3](../../board/GATES.md#gate-3--blind-prediction-of-a-single-block-without-the-dry-track-new-human-2026-10-18 "Gate 3 — blind prediction of a single block without the dry track (new, human 2026-10-18)") bars registered (proposed, not approved); Gate checklists)_
- 2026-10-25 · **Improvement.** Defaults are now pinned to a human-approved list (a file plus a test): knob fine-tuning is an option with the level knobs locked, and the provisional loss and the drive families are no longer defaults. Bundle approvals are recorded as provisional. _(Evidence: Approved-methods file and its pinning test; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-23 · **Improvement.** Overnight, training data for amp, cabinet, modulation, delay, reverb and equaliser was rendered and the predictors trained. On 18 held-out recordings from the two library players they name the exact model well (cabinet 76 percent, equaliser 82, modulation 75) and poorly on a new player (amp 19 to 31 percent). Knobs are modest. Trained, not verified; nothing is adopted into any matcher. _(Evidence: Overnight slot-dataset results note; Amp slot heads, held-out DI files; Cab slot heads, held-out DI files)_
- 2026-10-23 · **Improvement.** The amp 'silence' failures were explained (a fixed -90 dBFS render floor cut off genuinely quiet renders at low Master settings, mostly on preamps) and fixed by refilling 5,526 of 5,653 failed clips; 127 clips that are truly digital silence are left out. _(Evidence: Slot datasets: design and the amp silence diagnosis; Overnight slot-dataset results note)_
- 2026-10-23 · **Improvement.** Re-running the seven songs with the human's calibrated guitar lowered the listening-measure distance on 5 of 7 songs (clearly on 3). Label: listening-measure distance across different dry tracks; no ear verdict. The measure is not validated to order renders of different clean guitars, and notes, tone and level changed together, so no gain can be credited. The human wants to test the presets themselves. _(Evidence: Calibrated-guitar re-run on seven songs (distances); Knob fine-tuning on the seven picks (distances))_
- 2026-10-23 · **Decision that changed the plan.** The human approved a chain transfer test: about 300 random drive, amp and cabinet chains (plus optional effects) to see whether the slot predictors still name their block inside a chain. It ran the same day (see the result below). _(Evidence: State of the project, 2026-10-23 (with the corrections appended the same day))_
- 2026-10-23 · **New problem.** The chain transfer test answered no: on 295 finished chains the single-block predictors name the drive 2 percent and the amp 1 percent of the time (93 and 54 alone); cabinet 25, modulation 21, delay 10, reverb 28, equaliser 12 percent; knobs at a constant guess. A drive in front of the amp hurts the amp predictor and a cabinet after them nearly erases both. The 240,000 single-block renders cannot be used as chain shortlists as they are. The human approved a chain-trained pilot (about 5,000 chains, scored on the same test set), which is running. _(Evidence: Chain transfer test: slot predictors on 295 finished chains (drive 0.02, amp 0.01 exact model; measurement only); Chain transfer test note; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-23 · **Decision that changed the plan.** At the human's request the lead produced a project analysis from an independent methodology audit and two research surveys. Findings the lead accepts: no result since mid-October rests on a sealed blind test; the listening measure is used beyond its ear validation; several methods became defaults by reuse without itemised approval; 240,000 single-block renders preceded the chain check; the rebuilt dry's contribution is unmeasured. Ten itemised decisions are listed for the human. This report corrects the overstatements the audit found (one experiment heard rather than two independent tests; measured-pedal mode is simulation only; 18 held-out files rather than thousands of clips; one song and six decided trials rather than 'a whole chain can be tuned'; the admitted score is unapproved and at chance on hard pairs; slot predictors trained, not verified; [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") disowned by the human; every 'closer' figure labelled as a cross-dry-track distance with no ear verdict). _(Evidence: Project analysis, 2026-10-23 (lead): verdict, alternatives, ten decisions for the human; Methodology audit, 2026-10-23 (independent, read-only; evidence classes, overstatements found); Research note: blind full-chain estimation and advanced predictors; Research note: the dry-signal strategy (rebuild, effect removal, separation, level); Decision log)_
- 2026-10-22 · **Improvement.** A full drive search against a rebuilt clean guitar was run on seven songs; the human listened once, openly, side by side with the separated guitar, with no alternative candidate, and said 'those actually all sound pretty good' (evidence class C). A knob fine-tune lowered the listening-measure distance on 6 of 7 and the human heard 'the same or better' in the same open pass; Output and Master moved freely. _(Evidence: Seven-song dry-track drive search (distances); Knob fine-tuning on the seven picks (distances))_
- 2026-10-22 · **Improvement.** The human recorded a guided calibration session for their guitar. The profile (263 own notes, tone curve, normal playing level -36.1 dBFS) shows the real guitar plays 8 decibels quieter than the earlier stand-in level and is more mid-forward than the library guitars. _(Evidence: Guitar calibration profile note; Stand-in guitar level from 14 dry takes)_
- 2026-10-22 · **Improvement.** Basic Pitch was installed locally and its settings were swept against the human's dry takes; a clean guitar can now be rebuilt for all seven separated songs. Alignment to the stem was good on slow parts and unmeasurable on fast dense riffs. _(Evidence: Basic Pitch settings sweep against the human's dry takes; Rebuilt dry track for Mother, alignment to the stem)_
- 2026-10-22 · **Decision that changed the plan.** A chord list on Mother made the match more decisive (runner-up margin 0.023 against 0.002), but the human heard the plain version as better in one open comparison, so the chord-list rebuild stays an option. The distances quoted for it (0.706 against 0.629) carry the label: listening-measure distance across different dry tracks; no ear verdict beyond that one open preference. _(Evidence: Mother without chord data (distance 0.629); Chord-list rebuild note)_
- 2026-10-22 · **New problem.** Five blind song matches (no dry guitar) were heard as two 'pretty off', one the closest so far, two 'somewhat close'. The weak spots are Zero Amp Bass DI standing in for a fuzz and a single drive block standing in for a rig. _(Evidence: Human live verdicts on the five blind song matches; Five blind song matches note)_
- 2026-10-22 · **Decision that changed the plan.** The human approved a fixed slot order for chain prediction (drive, amp, cabinet, modulation, delay, reverb, equaliser, each optional) and a calibration step for each guitar before matching. _(Evidence: Training-data coverage inventory (no renders); Guitar calibration profile note)_
- 2026-10-21 · **New problem.** Swapping the listening measure for freely licensed alternatives (PANNs, DAC) fell short of the current one, so the current measure stays, with its licence question open. _(Evidence: Encoder swap measurement (AFx-Rep stays best))_
- 2026-10-20 · **Improvement.** A predictor-first drive matcher named the correct drive model first on 85 percent of 48 blind Helix targets (100 percent within three) in about 12 renders. On the human's own takes 67 and 88 percent. The human heard the first song match as pretty good. Evidence class D: the set was drawn by the search agent after the method was built, with no bar; the own-take cells are not statistically significant. _(Evidence: Predictor-first drive matcher, blind on 48 Helix targets; Human listen: Mother drive match "pretty good"; Predictor-first drive matcher note; Methodology audit, 2026-10-23 (independent, read-only; evidence classes, overstatements found))_
- 2026-10-20 · **Improvement.** A tuned version of a tutorial amp chain beat the tutorial settings in a short blind session (8 trials, 6 decided, 1 of 2 repeats consistent; mean rating 4.0 against 2.5) and in one live listen, on one song and one window. Directional only; it does not show that a whole chain can be tuned reliably by search. _(Evidence: DS-Sample chain tuning, blind ratings session (v6); Blind rating session v6 repeat consistency (1 of 2; 7 of 12 across sessions); Methodology audit, 2026-10-23 (independent, read-only; evidence classes, overstatements found))_
- 2026-10-18 · **New problem.** [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 2 did not pass: 6 of 7 checks on the 96 sealed items. Category 0.865 (was 0.802), model 0.760 (was 0.646), settings error 0.091, better than the random walk on both model and settings, consistency split passes. Ranking the true preset first fails at 0.9375 against 0.95 (was 0.990): six misses on amp, drive and one cabinet, which the middle baseline shares. Modulation and drive are weakest; cabinet and equaliser are perfect. The rule is unchanged and v2 is now seen. _(Evidence: [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 2 on all 96 sealed synthetic-v2 items: 6 of 7 checks pass (ranking the true preset first fails: 0.9375 against 0.95); Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Decision that changed the plan.** The human approved the next improvement round for [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 3: a gain-stage score term, drive and modulation specialists, and a fresh sealed synthetic-v3 (generated and sealed since). Synthetic-v2 is now spent: diagnosis only, never used to admit a change. _(Evidence: Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Improvement.** Specialists were adopted for four families: cabinet and equaliser (model 24 of 24 on development items against 18 of 24 for the general method), time-based effects (held-out model 14 to 19), amp (param error 0.224 to 0.120), and a dispatcher plus adapters that route each family to its specialist. [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 2 ran on all of them; category and model rose accordingly. _(Evidence: [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 2 on all 96 sealed synthetic-v2 items: 6 of 7 checks pass (ranking the true preset first fails: 0.9375 against 0.95); Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Improvement.** Parallel jobs now survive restarts (an item-level queue, workers grow and shrink with the host scaler), the Native host cap was raised from 6 to 8 on the human's instruction, and a false silence alarm on a very quiet true preset (item i062) was fixed; i062 stays in the set as a normal item. _(Evidence: Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **New problem.** [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 1 did not pass. On all 96 items: model 0.646, settings error 0.132, ranking 0.990 and settings better than the random walk pass; category 0.802 (bar 0.85) and model non-inferiority to the random walk (low end -0.083, needs -0.05) fail. The 80 untuned items agree. Strong families: drive, cabinet, compressor, delay; weak: modulation, reverb, amp. The next attempt uses a fresh set and per-family specialists. _(Evidence: [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 1 on all 96 known-answer items: 4 of 6 criteria pass (category accuracy and model non-inferiority fail); Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Decision that changed the plan.** The human directed a specialist per effect family, with single blocks first and chains built from analysis, no full-library test, trained weights may be published, and the cabinet-response library may be used for training. The 19 non-impulse-response recordings were removed from the library folder to the Trash. The pass rule was frozen before the run, and the baseline "random search" is now called the random walk. _(Evidence: Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Improvement.** Native is verified for [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)"): three engine bugs were fixed (baked controls, hosts in the other rounding mode, an amp state latch), repeatability is 216 of 216, nothing is unexplained, and an in-run sentinel now guards long jobs. Recorded misses (116 of 120 setting-truth pairs, 63 of 73 baked renders) are one-step rounding and input/output blocks. _(Evidence: Native setting check, repeatability, after the engine fixes: 216 of 216 renders within the line, worst -161.5 dB (the first run was 215 of 216, worst -97.4 dB); Native setting check, setting truth: 116 of 120 pairs, rule 2b 63 of 73; Native host canary: 15 of 153 fresh hosts opened in numeric mode B; zero unexplained results; Native in-run sentinel catches a forced numeric-mode drift; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **New problem.** The pedal verification found real sampling defects: unstable USB audio for about 0.7 s after start, amp state carried between renders, four models refused, and a slow in-place glide on some delay and reverb settings. A longer pre-roll fixed the first; a one-sample slip remains in about 1 render in 90. No pedal result counts until it is handled. _(Evidence: Pedal verification, sessions 1-3 on the old pre-roll (setting truth, repeatability, settle timing); Pedal verification, last full run on the longer pre-roll: setting truth 20 of 40; sentinel tripped on a real one-sample slip; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Improvement.** Search got measured seeding (the target's own delay time, reverb decay and modulation rate set the starting point) and then a hybrid method with broad draws, narrowing and refinement, chosen by a rule fixed before the last run. On the 16 development items model accuracy went from 6 to 13 of 16 over the day (development only, not gate evidence). _(Evidence: Hybrid search development runs on the 16 development items (not gate evidence); Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Improvement.** Baselines were recorded: the middle-of-every-knob guess and the random walk. The random walk scores high because it has 300 tries over a 47-model pool judged by the trusted score. _(Evidence: Baseline "middle of every knob", 96 items (provisional weights, before admission); [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 1 on all 96 known-answer items: 4 of 6 criteria pass (category accuracy and model non-inferiority fail); Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Improvement.** The curated preset corpus (2,068 presets after cleaning) gave strong order rules, super-groups and heavy concentration of model use; descriptive metadata adds little. Only category-level statistics are committed. _(Evidence: Preset-corpus chain statistics (category level only); Chain-pattern prior built from 129 presets (aggregate statistics only); Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Improvement.** The cabinet impulse-response library analysis shows that estimating a cabinet by deconvolution is promising: the pedal's cabinet is the top match in 22 of 24 real-clip tests when the amp is right. It is useless with the wrong amp. No library data leaves the machine. _(Evidence: Cabinet impulse-response library analysis (aggregate note, vendor-free); Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **New problem.** The block-presence detector, scored once on its test split, passed only for the cabinet and for whether a gain stage is present. It is used as a soft hint only; time-based blocks are confirmed by rendering with and without them. _(Evidence: Block-presence detector scored once on its test split (single Helix Native blocks); Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Resolved.** The two engine items that were held back (out-of-range values and a real-guitar smoke test) are done: the real-guitar smoke test passes on Native. On-demand capture defaults off after it opened an editor window per preset. _(Evidence: Native smoke test on a real guitar clip; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Resolved.** The Native verification suite first showed one of 216 repeat renders at -97.4 dB against the -100 dB line. The cause was a state carried between models (an amp latch), plus baked controls and a rounding mode. After three engine fixes repeatability is 216 of 216 (worst -161.5 dB), with an in-run sentinel in place. _(Evidence: Native setting check, repeatability, after the engine fixes: 216 of 216 renders within the line, worst -161.5 dB (the first run was 215 of 216, worst -97.4 dB); Native setting check, baked values: zero violations; Native in-run sentinel catches a forced numeric-mode drift)_
- 2026-10-17 · **Improvement.** A chain-pattern prior was built from 129 presets: drive comes before amp in 55 of 55 presets with both, amp before cab 90 of 90, delay before reverb in 96 percent. It is aggregate statistics only; no preset content is committed. It is not yet used by a chain builder. _(Evidence: Chain-pattern prior built from 129 presets (aggregate statistics only))_
- 2026-10-17 · **Decision that changed the plan.** New direction from the human: single blocks first, with chains built from analysis of real presets (which kinds of block are present, ordered by common patterns). The curated preset and cabinet-IR libraries stay local and are never committed; the song-metadata lookup (artist and title only) is approved as opt-in. _(Evidence: Decision log)_
- 2026-10-16 · **New problem.** Model choice tied the old pool pass at 7 of 16 development models under the old provisional score. This was superseded on 2026-10-17: the baselines are now recorded and the hybrid search replaced it. _(Evidence: Baseline "middle of every knob", 96 items (provisional weights, before admission); Decision log)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Improvement.** Native block fingerprints were measured for 602 of 605 library models (24 of about 3,600 settings were silent, all expected), and rendering many candidates at once is 1.46 times faster than one at a time with identical results. Model choice per slot is built. _(Evidence: Native block fingerprints: 602 models, 24 expected-silent settings)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Improvement.** The pedal's two-variant pass equals serial renders on firmware 3.80 and is about 1.7 times faster per variant (1.70 s against about 2.9 s). This closes the audio check that was open last report. Pedal restores are now judged by decoded equality, since raw bytes can differ by 3 after the pedal re-encodes. _(Evidence: Pedal two-variant pass equals serial renders, about 1.7 s per variant (firmware 3.80); Pedal restore judged by decoded equality (offline re-check of the hardware buffers))_
- 2026-10-16 · **Decision that changed the plan.** The human chose the [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") scoring rules (tune the measure for the known-recording case; count identical-sounding look-alikes as ties) and waived the 'right chain structure' target for [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)"), which now covers single blocks only. Chain structure moves to [Gate 4](../../board/GATES.md#gate-4--matches-clean-recordings-of-real-pedals-new-human-2026-10-17-was-gate-3-renumbered-2026-10-18 "Gate 4 — matches clean recordings of real pedals (new, human 2026-10-17; was Gate 3, renumbered 2026-10-18)"). Scoring rules 2 and 3 are coded. _(Evidence: Decision log)_
- 2026-10-16 · **New problem.** The first trust check on real Helix sound failed: no tone-comparison measure picks the true preset reliably. On new notes the best managed about 7 times in 40 against a bar of 36; the measures react more to the notes played than to the tone. With the same notes and a lined-up recording one measure reached 0.725 (0.875 if look-alikes that sound identical are ignored). This replaces the earlier stand-in result as the main evidence and is now the biggest problem. _(Evidence: Tone-comparison trust check on real Helix renders (40 known chains, 2,400 renders))_
- 2026-10-16 · **Improvement.** The software engine now holds 605 of the pedal's 607 effect models as single-block templates (the other 2 are refused by Native). Every template was replayed in a fresh host. This opens model choice from a library, but the blocks are not yet measured. _(Evidence: Native block library: 605 of 605 pedal models captured)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Improvement.** Delay times in the software engine now move in 10 ms steps instead of 63 ms, because Native honours a narrower control range. Block Level was also given a finer range (0.25 dB steps with 0 dB on the grid). _(Evidence: Narrower control ranges honoured by Native (delay Time in 10 ms steps); Block Level range \[-25.75, +6\] dB and re-captured templates)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Decision that changed the plan.** The measurements settled where the software engine can stand in for the pedal: amps, cabinets, dynamics, equalisers, most drives, delays, four reverb types, Hall above 500 Hz and the modulation effects. The pedal still checks Ganymede-type reverbs, Hall on bass-heavy material, Bitcrusher-type drives and Early Reflections. A suggested delay-time correction of under 1 in 100 was rejected: the apparent error was Native's control step, not an engine difference. _(Evidence: Time-effect agreement measured with test tones (reverbs, modulation, delays); Gray Flanger and Deluxe Phaser agreement on 20 s tones; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Improvement.** Two chains in one pedal pass give the same sound as one chain per pass on firmware 3.80, and code to render two chains per pass was written. Its audio comparison has not run because the Mac's audio failed during the session. Update: the audio comparison was later run and passed (see the 2026-10-16 entry on the pedal's two-variant pass). _(Evidence: Two-chain pass versus single-chain pass on the pedal, firmware 3.80; Pedal engine push: control-only session (two-chain writes, layout change, readback); Pedal session: every audio stream failed (CoreAudio down))_
- 2026-10-16 · **Improvement.** The separation stress curve is recorded on a synthetic mix: guitar-only quality 21.2 dB falls to 7.3 dB with a second guitar. This ticks one [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") item but says nothing yet about real songs. _(Evidence: Separation stress curve on a synthetic mix (five densities))_
- 2026-10-16 · **Decision that changed the plan.** The search keeps scoring the full clip: shorter clips did worse in the clip-length study. Two-chain passes on Native are out of scope (decided by the owner), and time-variant blocks are opt-in for search. _(Evidence: Clip-length study for the search; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Improvement.** Checking candidates on the pedal got much faster: a search trial went from 10.9 s to 3.8 s and the per-test session overhead from 3.81 s to 0.77 s. A verification finalist improved less, from 29.2 s to 24.7 s. The search now uses these options by default (task [T062](../../board/TASKS.md#t062 "Wire the T061 Stomp speed options into scripts/match_song.py as the…")), but how much that speeds a real search has not yet been timed on the pedal. These timings are on one pedal with one chain type; they say nothing about match quality. _(Evidence: Pedal search trial time, before and after; Pedal session overhead, before and after; Pedal verification time per finalist, before and after; Pedal speed measurements and the flash-slot proposal)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Improvement.** Helix Native's speed work ([T055](../../board/TASKS.md#t055 "Native phase 3: parallel render pool for search")) is finished. Capturing a new chain fell from about 31 s to 1.2 s in one session and 0.39 s with three sessions (216 blocks in 84.6 s). A pool of 6 parallel hosts runs 9.9 two-second tests per second (4.6 on ten-second clips); 10 hosts did a little better but with memory pressure and swapping, so the cap is 6. Bypass by MIDI control still matches separate captures (-328.7 dB), and a fault where about 1 host in 10 rendered differently is now caught by a start-up check. Measured on one Mac; nothing here has been judged by ear. _(Evidence: Native capture speed-up (216 chains); Native render pool, 2 to 10 hosts (6 is the cap); Native bypass over MIDI control re-checked; Native host canary: 15 of 153 fresh hosts opened in numeric mode B; zero unexplained results; Native engine notes, phase 3)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Improvement.** Follow-up Native work ([T064](../../board/TASKS.md#t064 "Native phase 3 follow-ups")): the two template sets used for matching (B and B0) were re-captured with Hum and Ripple at 0, so the matcher has all 7 test candidates under both engine policies. A scan of 607 pedal block models found only 2 that Native 3.82 refuses to load, so nearly the whole catalog can be captured for Native; the library itself is still not captured. _(Evidence: Native templates B and B0 re-captured at the frozen values; Refused-model scan: 2 of 607 pedal models refused by Native 3.82)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Resolved.** The pedal's two-path layout difference is fixed ([T063](../../board/TASKS.md#t063 "Two-path (Path A + B) render gap on the HX Stomp fw 3.80")). Our two-path version of a stored preset used to cancel against the one saved by the pedal's editor at only -9.3 dB; it now cancels at -85.7 dB, the same as the editor's preset repeated against itself. Single-path loads are unchanged. Measured on firmware 3.80, one preset, one pedal. _(Evidence: Two-path layout fix: -9.3 dB before, -85.7 dB after; Two-path pedal render differs from single-path (and snapshot recall check))_
- 2026-10-16 · **Decision that changed the plan.** The rule that two-path renders may only be compared with two-path renders is lifted, because after the layout fix a two-path pass matches single-chain renders (Path A -72.9 dB against a repeat floor of -68.7; Path B -52.6 against -42.7). Two chains per pass can be used for checks; the 2.0x gain was measured on firmware 3.50 and is not re-measured on 3.80. _(Evidence: Two-path layout fix: -9.3 dB before, -85.7 dB after; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Decision that changed the plan.** Decided by the owner: the amp's Hum and Ripple settings are frozen at 0 on both engines. They added a free-running noise that made two renders of the same preset differ at about -30 dB (against -72 to -91 dB at 0), which hid real differences and blocked the shorter pedal waits. This closes the open question and removes it from the owner's action list. _(Evidence: Pedal renders with amp Hum and Ripple on versus at 0; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Decision that changed the plan.** Decided by the owner: Helix Native runs at most 6 hosts at once (measured on 32 GB of memory: green memory pressure at 6, yellow with swapping at 8 and 10). All Native pool figures in this report are for 6 hosts. _(Evidence: Native render pool, 2 to 10 hosts (6 is the cap); Decision log)_
- 2026-10-16 · **Decision that changed the plan.** Decided by the owner: Native results from before 2026-10-16 are kept as history and not re-run wholesale. About 1 host in 10 had rendered differently and delay trims could differ by up to 30 samples. The results we rely on are re-measured in the parity spot checks and the [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") benchmark. _(Evidence: Native host canary: 15 of 153 fresh hosts opened in numeric mode B; zero unexplained results; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-15 · **Resolved.** The pedal's blocks no longer stay locked after a control session. The helper now ends each session the way the pedal's own editing program does; a person confirmed the blocks were editable afterwards on firmware 3.80, where the lock had still occurred before the fix. _(Evidence: Pedal blocks still locked on firmware 3.80 before the fix; Pedal blocks editable after a session (owner-observed, firmware 3.80); Pedal smoke test on firmware 3.80, session close answered)_
- 2026-10-15 · **Improvement.** The pedal now runs firmware 3.80. Its engine smoke test passes there, and 9 of 9 whole-preset writes (including amps with impulse-response cabinets and one block from each effect category) landed and the pedal was restored byte for byte. Pedal-versus-Native agreement has not yet been re-measured on 3.80. _(Evidence: Pedal smoke test on firmware 3.80, session close answered; Whole-preset writes on firmware 3.80, 9 of 9, pedal restored)_
- 2026-10-15 · **Improvement.** Helix Native now loads new sounds without a person at the keyboard and renders about seven times faster, and it stopped running out of memory. _(Evidence: Native memory and speed fix)_
- 2026-10-15 · **Improvement.** The pedal write is now checked parameter by parameter. This traced and fixed a bug that made a delay run far too wet when the owner played a preset live on the pedal. _(Evidence: Pedal write read-back check; Live check of the delay fix)_
- 2026-10-15 · **Improvement.** A first real song section was matched end to end and loaded on the pedal. The result was informal progress, not a validation. _(Evidence: Informal listening to the first real-song matches)_
- 2026-10-15 · **Improvement.** Rendering a chain one block at a time matched rendering it whole on a test chain, which opens the way to measuring blocks individually. _(Evidence: Block-by-block rendering test)_
- 2026-10-15 · **New problem.** A new problem was found: one cabinet setting sounds different on the pedal and in Native. Presets for the pedal keep it at 0 for now. _(Evidence: Pedal versus Native agreement on four presets; Decision log)_
- 2026-10-15 · **New problem.** The first real-song run showed the tone score is not reliable: its top two candidates were almost tied and the listener preferred one clearly. _(Evidence: Informal listening to the first real-song matches)_
- 2026-10-15 · **Resolved.** A silent bug that could reuse an outdated Native setup (and made one measurement look much worse than it was) was found and fixed; the false result was voided. _(Evidence: Decision log)_
- 2026-10-15 · **Decision that changed the plan.** The owner approved the per-block approach (measure and search block by block). It adds work before [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") but shrinks the search space. _(Evidence: Decision log)_
- 2026-10-14 · **Milestone.** [Gate 1](../../board/GATES.md#gate-1--both-engines-driven-by-code-end-of-week-1 "Gate 1 — both engines driven by code (end of week 1)") was signed off: both engines are driven by code and a generated preset loads on the pedal. _(Evidence: [Gate 1](../../board/GATES.md#gate-1--both-engines-driven-by-code-end-of-week-1 "Gate 1 — both engines driven by code (end of week 1)") sign-off record)_

### Activity that did not advance readiness

- 2026-10-26 · An unattended overnight job started rendering about 40,000 drive, equaliser and reverb chains (15 percent from the human's own session recordings) to train the product-chain predictor. At 2026-10-26 16:23 local: 37,998 chains done, shard 96 of 105, render stops by 20:00, then training. In progress; no result.
- 2026-10-25 · Dry-signal ablation measured (30 searches): the winning drive model stays the same on 5 to 6 of 7 songs under each alternative clean-guitar input; DS-Sample changes in every arm. Distances are not comparable; seven blind packs are built and unrated. No readiness gain until the ear rates them.
- 2026-10-18 · [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 3 met all 7 checks on the 96 sealed items: category 0.885, model 0.771, settings error 0.101, ranking the true preset first 0.979 under the owner's extended rule, better than the random walk on model and settings. The gate is not passed until the owner signs. Delay (5 of 12 exact models) is now the weakest effect. The same day the human judged the model 'garbage' and declared every lead-made method ruling behind it unapproved; the confirmation run and harness checks stay open.
- 2026-10-17 · A chain builder (tested with fake engines) and a blind A/B listening tool were built, and a local [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") explainer for a non-technical reader was added (kept off the public repository). None of these changes a gate on its own.
- 2026-10-17 · The tone comparison was admitted for single blocks when the clean recording is known. Tuned on a train split and scored once on a held-out split of 96 items, it ranked the true preset first 0.990 of the time against a bar of 0.90 (older measure 0.781), counting identical-sounding look-alikes as ties. The tuning score matched the test score, so it was not overfit. This replaces the failed first check as the main evidence for that case only. Update 2026-10-18: the human judged the resulting model unusable and the admission is unapproved; in blind rating on hard same-performance pairs the measure agreed with the ear 10 of 25 times (chance).
- 2026-10-16 · The known-answer harness (96 one-block presets) was built and a 3-item run made. The run is provisional and not gate evidence; its first version scored the true preset against itself and was voided and re-run.
- 2026-10-16 · Pedal engine work: a shared pedal lock with a device check, layout-changing loads written whole with read-back, and the Simple Delay Scale report explained (not a fault). A pedal session had control steps pass but audio fail (Mac audio down); the pedal was restored. These are not yet committed.
- 2026-10-16 · The continuous-integration build (the automatic test run on GitHub) was fixed in commit 07b66ed: one test that needs a local-only file is skipped when the file is absent. It changes no product capability.
- 2026-10-15 · The block measuring kit was finished offline. It has not touched Helix yet, so it does not advance readiness until it is run.

Neither came close to the target on stand-in signals, so they did not move [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") forward.

### Sound engines: Helix Native and the HX Stomp pedal

**Working.** Turn a candidate preset and a clean guitar clip into sound, so candidates can be compared. Native is fast and offline; the pedal is the real thing.

- **What works.** Both engines are driven from code on macOS. Native is verified for [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)"): exact settings apart from explained misses, 216 of 216 repeat renders, a sentinel against drift. The pedal two-variant pass, whole-preset writes and decoded-equality restores work.
- **What is missing.** Windows and Linux, stereo blocks, the cabinet Early Reflections setting, some reverb and drive types. Pedal verification is incomplete: four refused models, a slow glide on some settings, and a one-sample slip in about 1 render in 90.
- **Primary problem.** No pedal measurement counts until the residual slip is handled.
- **Next step.** Detect or re-take slipped pedal renders, then fix the refusals and the glide, then re-register and re-run the pedal rules.
- **Evidence confidence.** Native: hardware-measured on one Mac, 9,770 renders in the verification. Pedal: hardware, firmware 3.80, with recorded failures; ear not involved.
- **Detail.** [Helix Native engine](#helix-native-engine), [HX Stomp engine and hardware reamp](#hx-stomp-engine-and-hardware-reamp), [Engine interface and selection](#engine-interface-and-selection)

### Preset files and the block catalog

**Partially Working.** Read, write and validate the preset files the pedal and Native load.

- **What works.** Reference files round-trip exactly, models are read from the owner's own install, generated files load, and a chain-pattern prior built from 2,068 curated presets (category-level statistics only) shows strong order rules.
- **What is missing.** Building up to three snapshots from a match, and forcing snapshot values to equal block values inside the writer.
- **Primary problem.** A stale snapshot value can silently change the sound; the rule 'block values win' is applied in scripts but not yet in the writer.
- **Next step.** Move the snapshot synchronisation into the preset writer.
- **Evidence confidence.** Round trip tested on two reference files; one generated preset loaded on the pedal.
- **Detail.** [Preset generation and validation (.hlx, catalog import)](#preset-generation-and-validation-hlx-catalog-import)

### Preparing the song: guitar isolation and a clean reference guitar

**Partially Working.** Extract the guitar from the recording and supply a clean guitar recording to compare against.

- **What works.** Separation runs in seconds, sections are found and a library of clean guitar clips is indexed. A stress curve on a synthetic mix is recorded. Since 21 October: a clean guitar can be rebuilt from a separated song and a calibration profile of one guitar exists. The ablation shows the winning drive model mostly stays the same under other clean-guitar inputs.
- **What is missing.** Any quality score on real songs and any measurement of the clean-guitar choice's benefit (the rebuild has never been compared with a library clean guitar on the same search). The rebuild is approximate and the level convention is undecided.
- **Primary problem.** The rebuilt clean guitar's contribution is unmeasured by ear (seven blind packs unrated); separation quality on real songs is unknown, and the human has ruled out separator work.
- **Next step.** Human rates the dry-ablation packs.
- **Evidence confidence.** Synthetic mix only; one real song without a quality score.
- **Detail.** [Guitar separation and section segmentation](#guitar-separation-and-section-segmentation), [Reference DI library (dry signal)](#reference-di-library-dry-signal)

### Judging tone similarity: comparison measures and block measuring

**Partially Working.** Say how close two guitar sounds are, and describe how each block behaves.

- **What works.** Five comparison measures run. The lead admitted one (the multi-resolution spectral measure, version 2) for single blocks with a known clean recording (0.990 on a held-out split); the human has not approved it, judged the model built on it unusable, and on hard same-performance pairs it is at chance. The learned listening measure orders effect models on the same performance in agreement with the ear and is the de facto ranker. A block-presence detector exists (cabinet and gain stage pass; the rest miss). A cabinet-response estimator is promising. Block fingerprints exist for 602 models.
- **What is missing.** A measure for the no-recording song case; reliable detection of delay, reverb and modulation blocks.
- **Primary problem.** No measure is approved by the human; the learned measure ranks the final candidates as an interim default and has no ear validation against a separated guitar (its blind session was answered with a verdict, not ranks).
- **Next step.** Decide whether to score the stem-validation pack; the chain predictor may replace measure-led ranking if it works.
- **Evidence confidence.** Real Helix renders from Native; held-out splits scored once; ear agreement only for ordering models on the same performance; not real songs.
- **Detail.** [Loss kit (tone metrics)](#loss-kit-tone-metrics), [Block probe and fingerprints](#block-probe-and-fingerprints), [Block-presence detector](#block-presence-detector), [Cabinet estimation by deconvolution](#cabinet-estimation-by-deconvolution)

### Searching for the preset, the colour stage and the song-to-preset pipeline

**Partially Working.** Try candidate presets, score them against the target and return the best preset.

- **What works.** A hybrid method with family specialists (cabinet and equaliser, time-based, modulation, amp, gain) runs on Native. On 96 sealed items it named the category 88.5 percent and the model 77.1 percent of the time with settings error 0.101 (attempt 3). A chain builder exists, tested with fake engines only. A full drive search plus knob fine-tune is run on seven songs from a rebuilt clean guitar (heard once, openly). A measured-pedal mode is built and simulated only. Since 25 October: a bounded equaliser fit, a reverb candidate grid and comparison pages, and a blind reverb estimator (decay and mix from a stem's note tails) exist as pilots.
- **What is missing.** No reverb model and no colour stage in any matcher; the estimator reads a real tail on 2 of 7 stems. Only the drive slot is matched; the first blind listening of those matches was negative. Chain builder on a real engine, snapshots, a one-command tool.
- **Primary problem.** The first blind listening of drive-only matches on real songs was negative, and the reverb has no model.
- **Next step.** Read the chain predictor, then decide the reverb model with the human.
- **Evidence confidence.** Synthetic known-answer data (96 sealed items, [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") now closed as superseded) on Native; seven songs heard once openly and five songs once blind (negative); reverb estimator on 189 known reverbs; the pedal never probed.
- **Detail.** [Search and optimiser](#search-and-optimiser), [End-to-end song-to-preset pipeline and CLI](#end-to-end-song-to-preset-pipeline-and-cli), [Chain builder](#chain-builder), [Per-family specialists](#per-family-specialists), [Colour stage after the drive: EQ and reverb](#colour-stage-after-the-drive-eq-and-reverb)

### Testing, benchmarks and listening

**Partially Working.** Provide the measurements and listening tests that decide whether the product works.

- **What works.** The evidence ledger, smoke tests, engine agreement measurements, the 96-item known-answer set with its runner and frozen pass rule, three full attempt results (the third meets all checks on paper; the human judged the model unusable), a blind A/B session tool and seven rated short sessions. Since 25 October: a sealed 120-item [Gate 3](../../board/GATES.md#gate-3--blind-prediction-of-a-single-block-without-the-dry-track-new-human-2026-10-18 "Gate 3 — blind prediction of a single block without the dry track (new, human 2026-10-18)") set with a verifier and a scorer that refuses to run without a launch reference, a blind ranking-pack builder and scorer, and pre-registered pilots (reverb) scored by the script.
- **What is missing.** Approved [Gate 3](../../board/GATES.md#gate-3--blind-prediction-of-a-single-block-without-the-dry-track-new-human-2026-10-18 "Gate 3 — blind prediction of a single block without the dry track (new, human 2026-10-18)") bars and any scoring of the sealed set, a blind rating of the song matches that is scored, and sessions long enough to beat the listener's own repeat noise on pair trials (7 of 12).
- **Primary problem.** [Gate 3](../../board/GATES.md#gate-3--blind-prediction-of-a-single-block-without-the-dry-track-new-human-2026-10-18 "Gate 3 — blind prediction of a single block without the dry track (new, human 2026-10-18)") bars are proposed, not approved, so the sealed set cannot be scored.
- **Next step.** Human rules on the bars; benchmark scores once.
- **Evidence confidence.** Hardware measurements of engine agreement; full known-answer runs on Native ([Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)"), closed as superseded); sealed set built but unscored; listening short and directional.
- **Detail.** [Benchmark, ledger and gates (evaluation)](#benchmark-ledger-and-gates-evaluation), [Blind listening sessions and export](#blind-listening-sessions-and-export)

### Learning from data

**Partially Working.** Learn from rendered pairs to name a block and its settings, so the search needs fewer renders.

- **What works.** A drive predictor narrows the search (85 percent right first on 48 blind targets, evidence class D) and six more slots have trained predictors from about 240,000 single-block renders. An overnight job is rendering about 40,000 drive, equaliser and reverb chains to train a predictor of the product chain (37,998 done at 2026-10-26 16:23).
- **What is missing.** The chain predictor has no result; the slot predictors for other slots are out of scope and failed inside chains; nothing is verified on the sealed set.
- **Primary problem.** The open bet has no result yet, and single-block predictors do not transfer to chains.
- **Next step.** Let training finish, then read the seven-song table and propose how to score it.
- **Evidence confidence.** Measurement on Helix Native renders; the new job has produced no result; no ear test of any predictor-driven match.
- **Detail.** [Learning (predictor, proxies)](#learning-predictor-proxies)

### Foundations: installation, licensing and the working process

**Working.** Make the project installable, legally clean and organised.

- **What works.** Installs on this Mac, licence checks pass, and the board, evidence ledger and gates are in use. A file now pins song-match defaults to what the human approved.
- **What is missing.** Windows and Linux checks, recorded licence-agreement findings, and tidier board lines ([T223](../../board/TASKS.md#t223 "Blind validation of AFx-Rep against separated stems"), [T228](../../board/TASKS.md#t228 "Colour pilot (human 2026-10-25 \"focus on our distortion model… drive…") and [T230](../../board/TASKS.md#t230 "Reverb-model pilot (human 2026-10-25 \"yes\")") lines are behind the record).
- **Primary problem.** Licence-agreement findings are unrecorded; some board lines are stale.
- **Next step.** Update the board; owner records the licence findings.
- **Evidence confidence.** Tests and a licence scan; one operating system.
- **Detail.** [Platform, install and packaging](#platform-install-and-packaging), [Licensing and legal hygiene](#licensing-and-legal-hygiene), [Agent team, board and process](#agent-team-board-and-process)

## Engines: what each can do and how fast

Times are in seconds (s), minutes (min) and hours (h). A render is one pass of a test signal through a preset, recorded as audio. A score is one comparison of a rendered candidate against the target tone (lower is better). A capture is the one-off step that teaches Helix Native a new chain, by loading the preset file into its editor and recording its state. A parity check renders one preset on both engines and compares the two recordings. Estimates are 100 times the newest measured time; every estimate says which measurement it uses and its date. Native pool figures use 6 hosts (the owner approved that cap on 2026-10-16) and count a render plus its score as one test; pedal figures from firmware 3.80 are marked as such.

### What each engine can do

| Capability | HX Stomp (the pedal) | Helix Native (software) |
|---|---|---|
| Load a generated preset file (.hlx) directly | Yes. The file is compiled and written whole into the pedal's working memory, then read back parameter by parameter before anything is rendered; about 40 whole writes, all read back as written ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 Stomp T056 outcomes](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-stomp-t056-outcomes-lead "2026-10-15 (Stomp T056 outcomes, lead)")). 4 of 4 generated files were written and rendered (ledger [2026-10-14T23:47:43+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L75 "smoke · stomp · smoke · whole_preset_rendered = 4.0 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows)). Re-checked on firmware 3.80: 9 of 9 whole writes landed and the pedal was restored byte for byte (ledger [2026-10-15T23:40:20+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L235 "smoke · stomp · fw380-write · whole_preset_landed = 9.0")). Takes 0.53 s. | Yes, with no person, on macOS: a script drops the file into Native's editor and records the state as a template, then renders from the template (ledger [2026-10-14T23:58:32+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L88 "smoke · native · smoke · drop_load_null_vs_knob_db = -315.7 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)). A stored template loads in 0.15 s; a chain never seen before needs a capture first (see below). |
| Export or save a preset file | Our own writer produces the .hlx; a human imports it in HX Edit (accepted, ledger [2026-10-14T17:38:00+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L21 "smoke · - · known.hlx · hx_edit_import_ok = 1.0")). The engine never saves to the pedal's flash memory, by policy: it edits the working copy only and puts the original preset back ([DECISIONS 2026-10-14 stomp-engine, T003 hardware batch and T004](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-stomp-engine-t003-hardware-batch-and-t004-for-the-lead-to-confirm-at-plan-sync "2026-10-14 (stomp-engine, T003 hardware batch and T004; for the lead to confirm at plan-sync)")). Verified on fw 3.50, recheck on 3.80 pending. | Not done and not needed for matching: the generated .hlx is the source, and Native's captured state is kept as a local template, not exported as a file. |
| Change any parameter | Yes, at full resolution. Parameters are set in place and checked by read-back to within one hundred-thousandth of the parameter's range ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 stomp-engine, T051 whole-write readback](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-stomp-engine-t051-whole-write-readback "2026-10-15 (stomp-engine, T051 whole-write readback)")). Re-checked on firmware 3.80: 20 in-place loads of six random amp parameters each ended with a clean full read-back (ledger [2026-10-15T23:57:03+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L258 "render-cost · stomp · stomp-speed · param_load_s_readback_every1 = 0.1955 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). | Only parameters given a MIDI control number at capture, in 128 steps across each range; everything else stays as captured. Inside that grid it equals a freshly captured file almost exactly (ledger [2026-10-15T20:46:58+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L151 "parity · native · blockserial-v0 · delay_1ulp_sensitivity_null_db = -52.9 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). On the 8-block test chain 54 of 63 mapped parameters moved the sound (ledger [2026-10-15T21:31:30+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L179 "smoke · native · smoke · mod_chain_repeat_null_db = -332.5 (first of 5 rows at this time)") (5 rows)). |
| Change models (swap an amp, cab or effect) | Yes. Any model or layout change is one whole write with read-back, never the pedal's own model command ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 Stomp T056 outcomes](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-stomp-t056-outcomes-lead "2026-10-15 (Stomp T056 outcomes, lead)")). 0.53 s per write. On firmware 3.80, one block from each of the six effect categories and two amps with impulse-response cabinets were built and read back (ledger [2026-10-15T23:40:20+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L235 "smoke · stomp · fw380-write · whole_preset_landed = 9.0")). | Yes, from the block library: a model is loaded by its id from a stored template, with no new capture. Chains of several blocks still need a capture (0.56 s, once per chain). Mono only (ledger [2026-10-16T15:25:12+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L473 "render-cost · native · block-library-v1 · capture_per_chain_s = 0.563 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)). |
| Switch blocks on and off by script | Yes. Bypass is toggled in place with a MIDI control (the delay's bypass moved with CC 110, ledger [2026-10-15T21:14:14+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L162 "smoke · stomp · DS-Sample · delay_defaults_reproduced = 0.0 (first of 14 rows at this time)") (14 rows)), and is part of every whole write. Verified on fw 3.50, recheck on 3.80 pending. | Yes. Each block's bypass is a MIDI control number in the captured template; one capture reproduces every bypass pattern almost exactly (-328.7 dB against files captured one by one, ledger [2026-10-15T21:31:30+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L179 "smoke · native · smoke · mod_chain_repeat_null_db = -332.5 (first of 5 rows at this time)") (5 rows)). Only chains captured since 2026-10-15 have it; older templates keep fixed bypass states ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 native-engine, Native phase 2 block bypass](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-native-engine-native-phase-2-block-bypass-over-midi-cc "2026-10-15 (native-engine, Native phase 2: block bypass over MIDI CC)")). |
| Two chains at once (throughput) | Path A and Path B give twice the renders per pass (measured on firmware 3.50, ledger [2026-10-15T21:14:14+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L162 "smoke · stomp · DS-Sample · delay_defaults_reproduced = 0.0 (first of 14 rows at this time)") (14 rows); not re-measured on 3.80). The earlier gap of -18 to -22 dB between two-path and single-path renders was a layout bug, fixed by writing the layout the way the pedal's own editor stores it: on firmware 3.80 our two-path load now nulls against the editor-saved preset at -85.7 dB, equal to that preset's own repeat floor, against -9.3 dB before (ledger [2026-10-16T01:06:38+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L304 "parity · stomp · stomp-two-path · null_vs_hxedit_db = -9.3 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows)). A two-path pass also matches each chain rendered alone: Path A -72.9 dB (repeat floor -68.7), Path B -52.6 dB (floor -42.7). The rule that two-path renders are compared only with two-path renders was lifted on 2026-10-16 ([DECISIONS 2026-10-16 stomp-engine, T063 two-path layout](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-16-stomp-engine-t063-two-path-layout-for-the-lead-benchmark-and-search "2026-10-16 (stomp-engine, T063 two-path layout; for the lead, benchmark and search)")). Not tested: crossover and A/B split types; a pass with its own Path B input is refused by the compiler. Two-chain passes equal single-chain passes on firmware 3.80 for the tested chains (ledger [2026-10-16T15:44:03+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L595 "parity · stomp · twopath-parity-fw380 · null_residual_db = -72.9 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). The code that renders two variants per pass was then compared on the pedal's audio against serial renders: path A/B nulls of -78.5/-60.4 and -85.1/-78.6 dB, each below the serial repeat floor, about 1.7 s per variant (ledger [2026-10-16T20:28:58+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L697 "parity · stomp · render-many-stomp-fw380 · null_dual_vs_serial_db_KA_pathA = -78.48 (first of 6 rows at this time)") (6 rows)). | Out of scope: two-path passes on Native will not be built (owner's decision, 2026-10-16); Native renders in parallel on 6 hosts instead. Block-serial chaining equals one instance at -67 to -79 dB above 20 Hz on the tested chains (ledger [2026-10-15T19:21:10+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L134 "parity · native · blockserial-v0 · blockserial_null_db = -78.6 (first of 7 rows at this time)") (7 rows)). |
| Use the whole block and model catalog | In principle any compatible model can be written with no capture, because the pedal runs it itself. Tried so far: the amps, cabs, drives and delays in the test presets on firmware 3.50, and one block from each effect category and two amps with impulse-response cabinets on firmware 3.80 (ledger [2026-10-15T23:40:20+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L235 "smoke · stomp · fw380-write · whole_preset_landed = 9.0")). Whether the 90 models gated by firmware now work on 3.80 has not been rechecked ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 Stomp firmware upgrade, human](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-stomp-firmware-upgrade-human "2026-10-15 (Stomp firmware upgrade, human)")). Verified on fw 3.50, recheck on 3.80 pending. | Through the per-block library, now captured: 605 of the pedal's 607 models (the other 2, a transient shaper and a poly bass whammy, are refused by Native 3.82), each replayed in a fresh host (ledger [2026-10-16T15:25:12+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L473 "render-cost · native · block-library-v1 · capture_per_chain_s = 0.563 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)). Time-like and rate settings were re-captured with narrower control ranges (ledger [2026-10-16T16:04:28+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L638 "smoke · native · cc-range-v1 · delay_time_cc_step_ms_before = 62.99 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows), [2026-10-16T17:54:03+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L675 "smoke · native · cc-range-v2 · level_grid_null_db_worst = -136.4")). Not captured by design: routing models and the second cabinet of a dual-cab block. |
| Render speed | 3.49 s for a 2 s clip (the pedal plays the audio in real time); a 10 s clip is 10.7 s. Two-chain passes double throughput (see above). Measured on firmware 3.80: a 3 s clip takes 3.67 s (ledger [2026-10-15T23:48:50+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L242 "render-cost · stomp · stomp-speed · seconds_per_render_clip2s = 2.6889")). A search trial with the [T061](../../board/TASKS.md#t061 "Stomp speed improvements and assignment-free base") speed options takes 3.76 s (render side only). See the speed-up row below for the search and verification gains. | 0.17 s for a 2 s clip and 1.24 s for a 10 s clip including 3 s of pre-roll. A pool of 6 parallel hosts (the approved cap) finishes a search test of a 2 s clip, render and score together, in 0.10 s, and one of a 10 s clip in 0.22 s (timing tables below). |
| Snapshots | Snapshot values are written into the document and must equal the block values ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 snapshot values, lead on stomp-engine T051](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-snapshot-values-lead-on-stomp-engine-t051 "2026-10-15 (snapshot values, lead on stomp-engine T051)")). Switching snapshots by MIDI (CC 69) recalls the stored values (ledger [2026-10-15T21:14:14+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L162 "smoke · stomp · DS-Sample · delay_defaults_reproduced = 0.0 (first of 14 rows at this time)") (14 rows)). Switching during a render is not tested. Verified on fw 3.50, recheck on 3.80 pending. | Native renders the block values and ignores snapshot values when a file is loaded (ledger [2026-10-15T19:42:45+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L145 "parity · native · smoke · snapshot_stale_vs_block_db = -330.1 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). Snapshots are not switched in Native. |
| Agreement between the two engines | Measured on firmware 3.80 (ledger [2026-10-16T02:31:10+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L356 "parity · both · parity-spot-fw380 · null_residual_db = -37.45 (first of 15 rows at this time)") (15 rows), [2026-10-16T15:36:36+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L476 "parity · both · parity-timefx-stimuli-fw380 · rt60_max_abs_diff_pct = 6.494 (first of 10 rows at this time)") (10 rows), [2026-10-16T16:18:35+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L658 "parity · stomp · parity-timefx-stimuli-fw380 · sine330L_stomp_repeat_lfo_rate_diff_pct = -0.0756 (first of 14 rows at this time)") (14 rows)): amps cancel at -37.5 to -47.5 dB and cabinets at -35.4 to -37.4 dB (pass line -30 dB); dynamics and equalisers pass. For delays, reverbs and modulation the comparison is by echo timing, decay and modulation rate rather than cancellation. Accepted result: Native stands in for amps, cabinets, dynamics, equalisers, drives except Bitcrusher-type, delays, four reverb types, Hall above 500 Hz, and the modulation effects; the pedal still checks Ganymede-type reverbs, Hall on bass-heavy material, Bitcrusher-type drives and cabinet Early Reflections. A suggested delay-time scaling of under 1 in 100 was rejected (it was Native's control step). Verified on fw 3.50, recheck on 3.80 pending. (both engines) | |
| Reliability | The session-release lock is fixed ([T059](../../board/TASKS.md#t059 "Bug: pedal blocks stay locked after any control session")). Before the fix any control session left the pedal's block editing locked until USB was unplugged, even after the firmware 3.80 upgrade (ledger [2026-10-15T22:36:17+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L226 "smoke · stomp · session-release · blocks_locked_after_tonepush_info = 1.0")). The helper now sends HX Edit's session close, and a person confirmed the blocks were editable afterwards (ledger [2026-10-15T23:03:12+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L228 "smoke · stomp · session-release · blocks_editable_after_session = 1.0")); the close was answered on every channel in a later smoke run (ledger [2026-10-15T23:20:15+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L233 "smoke · stomp · smoke · engine_smoke_pass = 1.0 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). One earlier control wedge that needed a power cycle is still unexplained ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 HX Stomp priorities, human](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-hx-stomp-priorities-human "2026-10-15 (HX Stomp priorities, human)")). Pedal verification on 2026-10-17 (ledger [2026-10-17T17:37:28+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L825 "smoke · stomp · stomp-verify-v1 · check1_setting_truth_pass = 20.0 (first of 6 rows at this time)") (6 rows)): setting truth 20 of 40, repeatability 12 of 17; the audio stream is unstable for about 0.7 s after start, amps carry state between renders, four models are refused, and a one-sample slip remains in about 1 render in 90. Pre-roll is now 1.0 s (2.0 s with an amp). No pedal result counts until that slip is handled. | Memory growth and crashes are fixed: the host stays at about 3.0 GB and repeats are identical to -313 dB (ledger [2026-10-15T18:54:10+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L127 "render-cost · native · smoke · seconds_per_render = 0.282 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows); [DECISIONS 2026-10-15 native-engine, host memory](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-native-engine-host-memory "2026-10-15 (native-engine, host memory)")). About 1 host process in 10 rendered every chain differently until a start-up check (the host canary) began replacing such hosts (ledger [2026-10-16T00:25:27+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L280 "render-cost · native · smoke · renders_per_s = 8.426 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)); Native results from before 2026-10-16 are kept as history. Captures need a logged-in screen session. Verification on 2026-10-17 (ledger [2026-10-17T17:35:18+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L816 "parity · native · native-verify-v1 · repeat_pass = 216.0 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)): repeat renders 216 of 216 at worst -161.5 dB after three engine fixes (baked controls no longer sent, hosts in the other numeric mode re-prepared, an amp state latch at open); setting truth 116 of 120 pairs (ledger [2026-10-17T17:38:13+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L831 "parity · native · native-verify-v1 · truth_audit_pass = 116.0 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows)); a sentinel in the engine catches a forced drift (ledger [2026-10-17T17:29:36+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L812 "smoke · native · native-verify-v1 · sentinel_forced_drift_detected = 1.0")). Native results from before these fixes used the earlier engine. |
| Speed-ups since the last report | Measured on firmware 3.80 ([T061](../../board/TASKS.md#t061 "Stomp speed improvements and assignment-free base"), [docs/notes/stomp-speed.md](../../docs/notes/stomp-speed.md "HX Stomp speed (T061, stomp-engine, 2026-10-16, firmware 3.80)")): a search trial went from 10.861 s to 3.765 s (ledger [2026-10-16T00:17:11+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L274 "render-cost · stomp · stomp-speed · search_batch_per_trial_s_before = 10.861 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows), 10 trials, 3 s clips, readback every 10 loads, measured tail waits). Per-test session overhead fell from 3.812 s to 0.772 s (ledger [2026-10-15T23:47:32+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L240 "render-cost · stomp · stomp-speed · session_overhead_per_test_old_s = 3.8123 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). A verification finalist (whole write plus two 10 s renders) improved only from 29.23 s to 24.65 s (ledger [2026-10-16T00:10:35+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L272 "render-cost · stomp · stomp-speed · verify_batch_per_finalist_s_before = 29.2271 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). The shorter tail waits are only equal to the long waits with Hum and Ripple at 0 (ledger [2026-10-15T23:55:24+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L250 "render-cost · stomp · stomp-speed · tail_wait_bound_s_Cdly0 = 4.809 (first of 5 rows at this time)") (5 rows)). The search now uses these options by default (task [T062](../../board/TASKS.md#t062 "Wire the T061 Stomp speed options into scripts/match_song.py as the…")), but it has not been timed on the pedal. | Capture fell from about 31 s to 1.2 s per chain in one session, and 216 chains took 84.6 s (ledger [2026-10-15T23:57:11+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L261 "render-cost · native · block-library-v0 · capture_per_chain_s = 31.0 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows)). A pool of 6 hosts ran 9.9 tests per second on 2 s clips and 4.6 on 10 s clips (ledger [2026-10-16T00:38:03+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L295 "render-cost · native · smoke · renders_per_s = 4.639 (first of 5 rows at this time)") (5 rows)); 10 hosts ran 13.1 and 6.4 but with memory pressure and swapping, so the owner capped the pool at 6 on 2026-10-16. Newest batch capture: 391 single-block chains at 0.22 s each in 3 sessions with the parameter checks off (ledger [2026-10-16T00:58:52+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L302 "smoke · native · catalog-3.82 · native_invalid_models = 2.0 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). About 1 host in 10 rendered differently until a start-up check replaced it (ledger [2026-10-16T00:25:27+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L280 "render-cost · native · smoke · renders_per_s = 8.426 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)). The timing tables below read these newer rows. |
| Platforms | Tested on macOS only. Linux is expected to work and Windows is unconfirmed; both are set aside for now ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 HX Stomp priorities, human](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-hx-stomp-priorities-human "2026-10-15 (HX Stomp priorities, human)")). The pedal is now on firmware 3.80: its smoke test and whole writes pass there (ledger [2026-10-15T23:20:15+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L233 "smoke · stomp · smoke · engine_smoke_pass = 1.0 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)), but parity with Native and the firmware-gated models are not yet re-measured (spot checks [T060](../../board/TASKS.md#t060 "Parity spot checks: at least 5 models per block category on Stomp") are next). | Tested on macOS with Native 3.82 as a VST3 plug-in in a helper process. Other platforms untested. Real MIDI control into the AU build did not change parameters ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 native-engine, Native phase 2 block bypass](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-native-engine-native-phase-2-block-bypass-over-midi-cc "2026-10-15 (native-engine, Native phase 2: block bypass over MIDI CC)")). |

Each figure is the newest ledger row for that activity, with its date and a link to the row. Pedal results from the older firmware are marked until they are rechecked on 3.80; rows measured on 3.80 say so. Native pool figures use 6 hosts, the cap the human approved on 2026-10-16 ([docs/DECISIONS.md](../../docs/DECISIONS.md "Decision log")); results before that date are kept as history. The table regenerates from the ledger every run.

### Estimated time for 100 tests

Each estimate is 100 times the measured time above. Where a speed-up from running things in parallel has been measured, the second column shows it (Native: several engine hosts at once; Stomp: two-path passes, one pass rendering both Path A and Path B), with the assumption it rests on listed below the table. Numbers marked "not measured" have no measurement behind them.

| Activity | Stomp, one after another | Stomp, two-path passes | Native, one after another | Native, in parallel | Both or neither, one after another |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| One render of a 2 s test clip | 5.8 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 1) | 2.9 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 1) | 16.8 s (est., from 2026-10-16) (note 2) | 2.89 s (est., from 2026-10-16) (note 2) | not measured |
| One render of a 10 s clip | 18 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 1) | 8.9 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 1) | 2.1 min (est., from 2026-10-16) (note 3) | 21.6 s (est., from 2026-10-16) (note 3) | not measured |
| One comparison (score) of a candidate against a target | not measured | not measured | not measured | not measured | 5.35 s (est., from 2026-10-16) (note 4) |
| One parity comparison (per preset) | not measured | not measured | not measured | not measured | 47 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 4) |
| One engine smoke test | 22 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 4) | not measured | 12 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 4) | not measured | not measured |
| One search trial (render + score) | 6.4 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 5, 1) | 3.2 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 5, 1) | 58.7 s (est., from 2026-10-16) (note 6, 2) | 10.1 s (est., from 2026-10-16) (note 6, 2) | not measured |
| Verify one finalist on the pedal | 41 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 5) | not measured | not measured | not measured | not measured |
| A search run, per trial | 6.3 min (est., from 2026-10-16) | not measured | 84.6 s (est., from 2026-10-16) | not measured | not measured |
| One preset load or model change | 53.4 s (est., from 2026-10-15) | not measured | 15.0 s (est., from 2026-10-14) | not measured | not measured |
| First-time capture of a new chain | not measured | not measured | 56.3 s (est., from 2026-10-16) (note 7) | not measured | not measured |
| One probe analysis (offline) | not measured | not measured | not measured | not measured | 12 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 4) |
| **Combined: testing** | 22 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 4) | not measured | 12 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 4) | not measured | 1.3 h (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 4) |
| **Combined: comparison** | not measured | not measured | not measured | not measured | 5.35 s (est., from 2026-10-16) (note 4) |
| **Combined: modelling** | 6.8 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 1) | 3.9 min (est., from 2026-10-15) (note 1) | 78.4 s (est., from 2026-10-16) (note 2, 7) | 64.5 s (est., from 2026-10-16) (note 2, 7) | not measured |

Assumptions behind the estimates:

1. Stomp, in parallel means two-path passes: one pass renders two chains at once (Path A + B), so the throughput doubles. The gain of 2.0 was measured on fw 3.50; the dual-path-only comparison rule was lifted on 2026-10-16 after the [T063](../../board/TASKS.md#t063 "Two-path (Path A + B) render gap on the HX Stomp fw 3.80") layout fix (ledger [2026-10-16T01:06:38+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L304 "parity · stomp · stomp-two-path · null_vs_hxedit_db = -9.3 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows)), but the gain is not re-measured on fw 3.80
2. Native: 6 engine hosts at once (a render pool; the human approved a cap of 6 hosts on 2026-10-16), speed-up measured with 2 s clips as tests per second
3. Native: 6 engine hosts at once (a render pool; the human approved a cap of 6 hosts on 2026-10-16), speed-up measured with 10 s clips as tests per second
4. One after another: no parallel speed-up is measured for this activity (the benchmark steps run in sequence and a parity check needs the single pedal)
5. Stomp: one engine session serves the whole batch, so the session overhead is paid once, not once per test ([T061](../../board/TASKS.md#t061 "Stomp speed improvements and assignment-free base"))
6. Native: a search test is a render plus its score; the pool figures count both together (tests per second, 3 s pre-roll included), so no separate scoring time is added
7. Native captures are counted one after another at the per-chain time in the newest ledger row

**Takeaway.** The longest job today, one after another, is "Synthetic benchmark, Stomp recovery on all 60 items" at 9.5 h, most of it rendering (9.4 h); next is "Synthetic benchmark, full recovery (Native)" at 2.9 h. Parallel Native hosts cut "Synthetic benchmark, full recovery (Native)" from 2.9 h to 30 min; the Stomp has no such speed-up beyond two chains per pass. For Native, capturing is no longer the slow step when chains are captured in a batch: 0.56 s per chain in the newest row, against 1.24 s for a 10 s test; a single capture that includes engine start-up costs far more (see the measured table). 1 job has no measurement yet and shows "not measured".
