#### Helix Native engine

**Status:** working · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, hardware

**Purpose.** Render a DI clip through a candidate preset offline in Helix Native, fast enough to be the searching engine.  
**Done when.** A NativeEngine behind the frozen interface renders any chain structure the search needs at a measured cost, loads candidates without a GUI step per candidate, agrees with the Stomp within a measured null residual, and has run a full synthetic recovery pass.  
**Functionality today.** Loads an arbitrary generated .hlx into Native with no human (in-process file drop, about 66 s per new chain structure, once per machine; needs TONEMATCH_NATIVE_AUTOCAPTURE=1 and a logged-in GUI session), drives parameters through a MIDI-CC map, and renders a 2 s clip in about 0.28 s with memory flat near 3.0 GB. Renders are history-independent and match each file's own capture at -317 dB. Mono in and out only; chains with an enabled delay or reverb need a 3 s pre-roll; Captures of new single-block chains take 0.22 s each when batched across three sessions with the parameter checks off (ledger [2026-10-16T00:58:52+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L302 "smoke · native · catalog-3.82 · native_invalid_models = 2.0 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)), and a pool of 6 hosts renders and scores about 10 two-second tests per second. Windows and Linux are untested. Single-block templates for 605 pedal models load by model id with no capture (ledger [2026-10-16T15:25:12+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L473 "render-cost · native · block-library-v1 · capture_per_chain_s = 0.563 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)). Verified on 2026-10-17: settings land exactly apart from recorded misses, and renders repeat to -161.5 dB.  
**Progress note.** Hands-off loading (file drop plus MIDI-CC map), state-based reset renders (0.28 s, bit-identical to dedicated captures at -317 dB, flat 3.0 GB), stale-template protection and a memory watchdog are built, hardware-measured on one Mac, and exercised by a 449-render real-song search. Block-serial rendering is shown to equal one instance above 20 Hz (-67 to -79 dB). Still missing: Windows and Linux (the drop route is macOS VST3 only), stereo and dual-cab paths in the host, probe fingerprints, and any full synthetic recovery pass. Quality of what Native renders versus the pedal is only partly in parity (see evaluation), and nothing it renders has been blind-tested by ear. Phase 3 is finished ([T055](../../board/TASKS.md#t055 "Native phase 3: parallel render pool for search"), [T064](../../board/TASKS.md#t064 "Native phase 3 follow-ups")): capture takes 1.2 s per chain in one session and 0.39 s with three, instead of 31 s (ledger [2026-10-15T23:57:11+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L261 "render-cost · native · block-library-v0 · capture_per_chain_s = 31.0 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows)); a render pool of 6 hosts (the cap the human approved on 2026-10-16) runs 9.9 two-second tests per second and 4.6 ten-second tests per second, each test being a render plus its score (ledger [2026-10-16T00:38:02+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L294 "render-cost · native · smoke · renders_per_s = 9.879")); block bypass by CC matches separate captures at -328.7 dB (ledger [2026-10-16T00:29:34+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L285 "parity · native · smoke · bypass_patterns_cc_vs_drop_truth_db = -328.7")); a rejected model load is detected and closed; about 1 host in 10 that rendered differently is replaced by a start-up check; templates B and B0 are re-captured at the frozen values (ledger [2026-10-16T00:50:49+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L300 "render-cost · native · templates-B-B0 · capture_per_chain_s = 9.51 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)); and a scan of 607 Stomp block models found 2 that Native 3.82 refuses (ledger [2026-10-16T00:58:52+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L302 "smoke · native · catalog-3.82 · native_invalid_models = 2.0 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). The block library is now captured: 605 of 605 Stomp models (ledger [2026-10-16T15:25:12+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L473 "render-cost · native · block-library-v1 · capture_per_chain_s = 0.563 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)); delay times move in 10 ms steps because Native honours a narrowed CC range (ledger [2026-10-16T16:04:28+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L638 "smoke · native · cc-range-v1 · delay_time_cc_step_ms_before = 62.99 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows)), and Block Level has a finer range (ledger [2026-10-16T17:54:03+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L675 "smoke · native · cc-range-v2 · level_grid_null_db_worst = -136.4")). Written but not yet committed: render_many on the pool, a shared time-variant flag and small fixes; block fingerprints ([T047](../../board/TASKS.md#t047 "started 2026-10-16 (native-engine, engine push N2)")) are in progress. Measured on one Mac only; nothing here has been checked by ear. Results from before 2026-10-16 are kept as history (human decision). 2026-10-17: native-verify-v1 ([T103](../../board/TASKS.md#t103 "Native verification suite scripts/native_verify.py")) first results: baked-value violations 0 (ledger [2026-10-17T16:29:41+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L743 "smoke · native · native-verify-v1 · baked_violations = 0.0")); repeatability 215 of 216 renders within the line, worst -97.4 dB against -100 dB, so that check has not passed as registered (ledger [2026-10-17T16:34:08+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L744 "parity · native · native-verify-v1 · repeat_pass = 215.0 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). Fingerprints exist for 602 models (ledger [2026-10-16T19:13:32+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L692 "smoke · native · render-many-v1 · render_many_vs_serial_null_db_worst = -306.0 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows)). E3 (out-of-range values) and E6 (real-guitar smoke DI) are in progress; on-demand capture defaults to off. 2026-10-17 ([T103](../../board/TASKS.md#t103 "Native verification suite scripts/native_verify.py")): verified for [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)"). Three engine fixes: baked controls are not sent, hosts that open in the other numeric mode are re-prepared in place, and the first amp loaded is latched at open. Repeatability 216 of 216; an in-run sentinel exists; unverifiable models are excluded from search pools; on-demand capture defaults off.

**Challenges and findings**

- Host memory grew 4 to 11 GB and was killed: pedalboard treats Native as keeping audio on reset, so every reset render re-created the plugin and Native never freed an instance (+353 MB each). Fixed by resetting by state; renders are now 0.28 s instead of about 2 s and bit-identical to dedicated captures. Scores from runs before 2026-10-15T18:54 are not exactly reproducible (history-dependent at -62 to -72 dB). _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-15 native-engine host memory](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-native-engine-host-memory "2026-10-15 (native-engine, host memory)"); ledger [2026-10-15T18:54:10](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L127 "render-cost · native · smoke · seconds_per_render = 0.282 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)]_
- The old and new reset paths disagree on randomly set parameters (levels 11.6 to 14.4 dB apart). The new path matches each file's own drop capture, so it is believed correct, but this is not independently confirmed. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-15 native-engine host memory](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-native-engine-host-memory "2026-10-15 (native-engine, host memory)") (open question)]_
- A stale template was reused for a second file with the same chain structure but a different unmapped parameter, which produced a false parity row (voided). Templates are now keyed by structure plus a hash of baked values and legacy templates are refused. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-15 stale Native template](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-stale-native-template-lead "2026-10-15 (stale Native template, lead)"); [ledger/void.csv](../../ledger/void.csv "ledger/void.csv")]_
- SUPERSEDED 2026-10-16 ([T082](../../board/TASKS.md#t082 "N6 engine push: (a) exact 0.0 command lands at lo,"): the host's state reset clears every tail at any pre-roll, -291 to -316 dB; DECISIONS): Native's reset does not clear delay lines: a chain with an enabled delay alternates between two renders (-19.7 dB) at the default 0.1 s pre-roll; 3 s fixes it (-62 to -65 dB). _[ledger [2026-10-15T00:16:00+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L91 "render-cost · native · smoke · autocapture_s = 27.9 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)]_
- Block-serial: the -22.6 dB untrimmed result is an artefact of each instance's 16-sample delay against modulation; trimming 16 samples per instance gives -67.1 dB above 20 Hz, but the full band is limited to -50 dB by sub-5 Hz content from the delay. An Amp+Cab block is not exactly an amp block followed by a cab block (0.021 dB, -72.7 dB gain-matched). An unexplained -67 dB floor appears once the amp follows other blocks. Dual cab, stereo paths and non-default settings are not tested. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-15 T045](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-native-engine-t045-block-serial-spike "2026-10-15 (native-engine, T045 block-serial spike)"); ledger [2026-10-15T19:21:10](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L134 "parity · native · blockserial-v0 · blockserial_null_db = -78.6 (first of 7 rows at this time)") (7 rows) blockserial-v0]_
- Native applies a drop-loaded file's block values and ignores its stale snapshot values (-330.1 dB versus -38.3 dB); capture now syncs snapshots to blocks. A block-bypass MIDI-CC encoding Native honours has not been found, so bypass patterns need one capture each. _[ledger [2026-10-15T19:42:45](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L145 "parity · native · smoke · snapshot_stale_vs_block_db = -330.1 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows); [DECISIONS 2026-10-14 T018n](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-native-engine-t018n-hands-off-native-loading-proposals-for-the-lead "2026-10-14 (native-engine, T018n hands-off Native loading; proposals for the lead)") stage 2]_
- Whole-preset state write is impossible (opaque encrypted records); the file drop is the supported route. The human clicked 'Change HC mode' once and ticked 'Don't ask again' to get a clean start state; no state edits were adopted. _[[docs/notes/native-spike.md](../../docs/notes/native-spike.md "T002 / T013 Native spike: can a whole preset be written into Helix Native's state?"); [DECISIONS 2026-10-14 human rulings](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-human-rulings-on-native-recorded-by-lead "2026-10-14 (human rulings on Native, recorded by lead)")]_
- The Native installer EULA is silent on training ML on Native renders; the human marked the EULA reading done but recorded no finding. _[[docs/notes/line6-eula-flags.md](../../docs/notes/line6-eula-flags.md "Line 6 EULA flags for automation and protocol use (not legal advice)"); [DECISIONS 2026-10-14 project licence](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-project-licence-lead-at-the-humans-request "2026-10-14 (project licence, lead, at the human's request)")]_
- About 1 Native host process in 10 rendered every chain differently for its whole life (reverbs and modulated delays by -21 to -27 dB; parameters and saved state identical; cause unknown). A start-up check now replaces such hosts. Latency trims could also differ by up to 30 samples between engines; fixed by measuring once per engine with every block bypassed. _[ledger [2026-10-16T00:25:27+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L280 "render-cost · native · smoke · renders_per_s = 8.426 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows); [DECISIONS 2026-10-16 native-engine Native phase 3](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-16-native-engine-t064-native-phase-3-follow-ups-for-the-lead-search-benchmark "2026-10-16 (native-engine, T064 Native phase 3 follow-ups; for the lead, search, benchmark)")]_

**Still to do**

- Stereo-capable host path (the host renders mono in and folded mono out) before stereo blocks can be serialised
- Find a block-bypass CC encoding Native honours, or extend the template key with the enabled pattern
- Linux via Wine and yabridge and Windows are untested
- [T047](../../board/TASKS.md#t047 "started 2026-10-16 (native-engine, engine push N2)") block-probe fingerprints in Native; [T080](../../board/TASKS.md#t080 "N3 engine push: NativeEngine.render_many(dry, variants) on…") render_many on the pool; [T081](../../board/TASKS.md#t081 "N4 engine push: native_library.is_time_variant(model_id)") shared time-variant flag; [T082](../../board/TASKS.md#t082 "N6 engine push: (a) exact 0.0 command lands at lo,") small items (committed by the lead after review)

**Open blockers**

- Misses recorded as rule failures (7 one-step rounding landings, 7 input/output blocks) are explained and do not block [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)").

**Decisions**

- Native route: file drop plus MIDI-CC map, autocapture default off ([DECISIONS 2026-10-14 human rulings on Native](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-human-rulings-on-native-recorded-by-lead "2026-10-14 (human rulings on Native, recorded by lead)"))
- pedalboard (GPLv3) stays behind a process boundary ([DECISIONS 2026-10-13 cycle 1](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-13-cycle-1-mode-1 "2026-10-13 (cycle 1, mode 1)"))
- Host resets by state, memory watchdog at 5120 MB ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 host memory](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-native-engine-host-memory "2026-10-15 (native-engine, host memory)"))
- Block-serial corrections: trim 16 samples per instance, judge above 20 Hz, store amps and cabs as separate blocks ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 T045](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-native-engine-t045-block-serial-spike "2026-10-15 (native-engine, T045 block-serial spike)"))
- 3 s pre-roll for chains with delay or reverb ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15, lead](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-pedal-lockups-lead "2026-10-15 (pedal lockups, lead)"))
- Native host cap is 6; Hum and Ripple frozen at 0 on both engines; Native results from before 2026-10-16 are kept as history ([DECISIONS 2026-10-16 human, Native phase 3 follow-ups](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-16-human-recorded-by-tone-matcher-lead-native-phase-3-follow-ups-and-humripple "2026-10-16 (human, recorded by tone-matcher-lead): Native phase 3 follow-ups and Hum/Ripple"))
- Two-path passes on Native are out of scope (human, DECISIONS 2026-10-16)
- Block Level CC range \[-25.75, +6\] dB and optional Engine.render_many (lead, DECISIONS 2026-10-16)

**Risks**

- PLAN risk: Native EULA silent on ML training: findings of the human's EULA reading not recorded
- PLAN risk: Native on Linux and Windows (the drop route is macOS only)
- Native and Stomp differ in at least one parameter (cab Early Reflections); see evaluation
- Scores and parity rows from before the host-reset fix and the 2026-10-16 host canary are not exactly reproducible (about 1 host in 10 rendered differently); they stay as history and the ones we rely on are re-measured
- Native does not stand in for Ganymede-type reverbs, Hall on bass-heavy material, Bitcrusher-type drives or cabinet Early Reflections ([T066b](../../board/TASKS.md#t066b "Parity probe session closing the T066 open questions") ruling)

#### HX Stomp engine and hardware reamp

**Status:** working · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, hardware

**Purpose.** Drive the real pedal from code and capture its USB audio, so the final check is the actual hardware and hardware-only users have an engine.  
**Done when.** A StompEngine renders any candidate within the pedal's capabilities on macOS, Windows and Linux, with edit-buffer-only control, a calibrated reamp path, parity to Native measured, and a user-DI accuracy mode used on real songs.  
**Functionality today.** Writes a whole generated preset into the pedal's edit buffer (about 0.3 s), reads back every parameter and refuses to render a mismatch, reamps a DI over USB 5/6 to 1/2 (2.7 s for a 2 s clip, 3.7 s for 3 s), ends each session with HX Edit's session close so the pedal's blocks stay editable, and restores the original preset byte for byte. Verified on firmware 3.80 and macOS only; whole_preset is off by default; layout changes in place are unsupported; two-path (Path A plus B) loads use the editor's own layout and match single-chain renders (Path A -72.9 dB against a repeat floor of -68.7, Path B -52.6 against -42.7; ledger [2026-10-16T01:06:38+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L304 "parity · stomp · stomp-two-path · null_vs_hxedit_db = -9.3 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows)), with the 2.0x two-chains-per-pass gain measured on firmware 3.50 only.  
**Progress note.** Control, reamp, latency, restore and whole-preset injection with a parameter-by-parameter readback on every load are verified on one pedal and one OS (macOS), now on firmware 3.80 (the pedal was upgraded on 2026-10-15). The session-release lock is fixed: the helper now ends a session the way HX Edit does, and blocks stay editable (ledger [2026-10-15T23:03:12+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L228 "smoke · stomp · session-release · blocks_editable_after_session = 1.0"), [2026-10-15T23:20:15+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L233 "smoke · stomp · smoke · engine_smoke_pass = 1.0 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). The engine smoke test passes on 3.80, 9 of 9 whole-preset writes landed and the pedal was restored byte for byte (ledger [2026-10-15T23:40:20+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L235 "smoke · stomp · fw380-write · whole_preset_landed = 9.0")). Speed work ([T061](../../board/TASKS.md#t061 "Stomp speed improvements and assignment-free base")): a search trial went from 10.9 s to 3.8 s and per-test session overhead from 3.81 s to 0.77 s (ledger [2026-10-16T00:17:11+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L274 "render-cost · stomp · stomp-speed · search_batch_per_trial_s_before = 10.861 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows), [2026-10-15T23:47:32+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L240 "render-cost · stomp · stomp-speed · session_overhead_per_test_old_s = 3.8123 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). The two-path layout difference is fixed ([T063](../../board/TASKS.md#t063 "Two-path (Path A + B) render gap on the HX Stomp fw 3.80")): our two-path load of a stored preset nulled at -9.3 dB against the editor-saved one and now nulls at -85.7 dB, equal to that preset's own repeat floor (ledger [2026-10-16T01:06:38+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L304 "parity · stomp · stomp-two-path · null_vs_hxedit_db = -9.3 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows)); single-path loads are unchanged. Hum and Ripple are frozen at 0 on both engines (human decision, 2026-10-16). Still missing: Windows and Linux, the play-along path on hardware, the whole_preset default (still off), the Early Reflections mismatch with Native, and any speed timing of the search that now uses the options. Parity with Native on 3.80 has not been run. 2026-10-16: two-path passes equal single-path passes on firmware 3.80 (ledger [2026-10-16T15:44:03+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L595 "parity · stomp · twopath-parity-fw380 · null_residual_db = -72.9 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)). Engine push [T090](../../board/TASKS.md#t090 "S3 engine push: device check")-[T095](../../board/TASKS.md#t095 "Engine-push pedal session"): a shared pedal lock with a device check ([T090](../../board/TASKS.md#t090 "S3 engine push: device check")), two renders per pass ([T091](../../board/TASKS.md#t091 "S1 engine push: StompEngine.render_many")), layout-changing loads written whole ([T092](../../board/TASKS.md#t092 "S2 engine push: layout-changing loads through the whole-preset…")), the Simple Delay Scale report explained ([T093](../../board/TASKS.md#t093 "S4 engine push: Simple Delay Scale reported missing on 11C")) and a whole-preset default proposal ([T094](../../board/TASKS.md#t094 "S5 engine push: whole_preset default proposal")) are written; the control-only pedal session passed and restored 04C, but every CoreAudio stream failed so the audio comparison did not run (ledger [2026-10-16T18:30:48+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L688 "smoke · stomp · smoke · restore_bytes_equal = 1.0 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)). Not all of it is committed. 2026-10-16/17: the two-variant pass equals serial renders on the pedal (ledger [2026-10-16T20:28:58+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L697 "parity · stomp · render-many-stomp-fw380 · null_dual_vs_serial_db_KA_pathA = -78.48 (first of 6 rows at this time)") (6 rows), about 1.7 s per variant) and restores are judged by decoded equality ([T097](../../board/TASKS.md#t097 "E4: judge pedal restores by decoded equality"), ledger [2026-10-17T00:31:58+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L742 "smoke · stomp · smoke · restore_decoded_equal = 1.0")). Pedal-side verification (stomp-verify-v1) is running; it has no ledger row yet. 2026-10-17 ([T108](../../board/TASKS.md#t108 "HX Stomp verification suite scripts/stomp_verify.py")): the pedal verification found real defects: the USB audio stream is not stable for about 0.7 s after start, amps carry state from the previous render, four models are refused in whole-preset writes, some time parameters glide slowly in place, and a one-sample slip remains in about 1 render in 90. Pre-roll is now 1.0 s (2.0 s with an amp). No pedal result counts until [T112](../../board/TASKS.md#t112 "Residual one-sample USB slips") lands. The human confirmed the pedal blocks are editable.  
**Board tasks.** 18 of 22 done, 4 in progress: [T003](../../board/TASKS.md#t003 "Spike: fretwire on Linux drives a block, model and parameter change"), [T004](../../board/TASKS.md#t004 "Measure USB round-trip latency with an impulse"), [T014](../../board/TASKS.md#t014 "Pedal batch H1-H11 from docs/notes/T003-stomp-control-spike.md"), [T018s](../../board/TASKS.md#t018s "Spike: autonomous whole-preset injection on the HX Stomp"), [T019](../../board/TASKS.md#t019 "Own Stomp engine-smoke row once the human approves running the local…"), [T020a](../../board/TASKS.md#t020a "Stomp firmware 3.50 vs 3.80"), [T042](../../board/TASKS.md#t042 "Play-along user-DI capture"), [T051](../../board/TASKS.md#t051 "Bug: rank2_H_live delay read back Mix 0.72 / Feedback 0.52 / Time…"), [T061](../../board/TASKS.md#t061 "Stomp speed improvements and assignment-free base"), [T063](../../board/TASKS.md#t063 "Two-path (Path A + B) render gap on the HX Stomp fw 3.80"), [T071](../../board/TASKS.md#t071 "Two-path parity on the Stomp"), [T090](../../board/TASKS.md#t090 "S3 engine push: device check"), [T091](../../board/TASKS.md#t091 "S1 engine push: StompEngine.render_many"), [T092](../../board/TASKS.md#t092 "S2 engine push: layout-changing loads through the whole-preset…"), [T093](../../board/TASKS.md#t093 "S4 engine push: Simple Delay Scale reported missing on 11C"), [T094](../../board/TASKS.md#t094 "S5 engine push: whole_preset default proposal"), [T095](../../board/TASKS.md#t095 "Engine-push pedal session"), [T097](../../board/TASKS.md#t097 "E4: judge pedal restores by decoded equality"), [T108](../../board/TASKS.md#t108 "HX Stomp verification suite scripts/stomp_verify.py"), [T110](../../board/TASKS.md#t110 "Device refuses whole documents of VIC Stutter Edit, L6SPB Inf…"), [T111](../../board/TASKS.md#t111 "In-place time-parameter glide"), [T112](../../board/TASKS.md#t112 "Residual one-sample USB slips")

**Challenges and findings**

- The pedal's blocks stayed locked after any control session (ours and unmodified upstream tonepush, on firmware 3.50 and 3.80). Cause: no HX Edit-style session close. Fixed in the helper; blocks editable afterwards, close acknowledged on control and data channels (events not acknowledged), no USB reset needed. _[ledger [2026-10-15T22:36:17+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L226 "smoke · stomp · session-release · blocks_locked_after_tonepush_info = 1.0") (still locked on 3.80 before the fix); ledger [2026-10-15T23:03:12+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L228 "smoke · stomp · session-release · blocks_editable_after_session = 1.0"); ledger [2026-10-15T23:20:15+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L233 "smoke · stomp · smoke · engine_smoke_pass = 1.0 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows); [DECISIONS 2026-10-15 stomp-engine T059](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-stomp-engine-t059-session-release-phases-1-and-2-for-the-lead "2026-10-15 (stomp-engine, T059 session release, phases 1 and 2; for the lead)")]_
- The pedal recalls the current snapshot's controller values over the block values on load. The compiler used to keep the base document's snapshot values, so a generated preset's delay ran Feedback 0.52 instead of 0.40 and the human found it 'way too much' live. Fixed in the compiler (ledger 19:36:29); every load is now verified. _[DECISIONS 2026-10-15 stomp-engine T051; ledger [2026-10-15T19:20:34](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L133 "ab · stomp · DS-Sample · live_pref_delay_mix = 0.1")]_
- Delay Mix 0.72 and Time 0.47 read back from the pedal are in neither the file nor the base document. Unexplained; the hardware re-check after the fix is pending. The only results affected are the live listening session and the never-run H parity. _[board [T051](../../board/TASKS.md#t051 "Bug: rank2_H_live delay read back Mix 0.72 / Feedback 0.52 / Time…"); DECISIONS 2026-10-15]_
- Whole-preset injection works with audio (4 of 4), but 2 of 4 pre-registered predictions were wrong or inconclusive (crest wrong, delay tail inconclusive) and the first render of one preset was an outlier (-27.9 versus -40.3 dB), so whole_preset stays off. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 T018s rendered](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-stomp-engine-t018s-rendered-for-the-lead "2026-10-14 (stomp-engine, T018s rendered; for the lead)"); ledger 2026-10-14T23:47]_
- Terminal needs Microphone permission for reamping: without it captures are digital zero. HX Edit holds the control interface exclusively and must be quit during runs. _[[docs/DEV-ENV.md](../../docs/DEV-ENV.md "Dev environment notes"); [docs/notes/T003-stomp-control-spike.md](../../docs/notes/T003-stomp-control-spike.md "T003 spike: driving the HX Stomp from code (stomp-engine, 2026-10-13)")]_
- Hardware rendering is serial and real time, so recovery over 60 items is about 10 h and a CI run on hardware is not practical. _[[docs/notes/synthetic-benchmark-design.md](../../docs/notes/synthetic-benchmark-design.md "Synthetic recovery set v1: design, cost, capture request, proposed thresholds (T024)")]_
- stompd source is intentionally not in the repo, so Stomp results cannot be reproduced by others yet. _[[docs/notes/stompd.md](../../docs/notes/stompd.md "stompd: the HX Stomp control helper (stomp-engine, 2026-10-14)")]_
- With amp Hum and Ripple free, two Stomp renders of the same chain differ at about -30 dB (the hum runs free); with both at 0 the repeats are -72 to -91 dB. Tail-wait shortening is only safe with both at 0. Decided 2026-10-16: both are frozen at 0 on both engines. _[ledger [2026-10-15T23:55:24+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L250 "render-cost · stomp · stomp-speed · tail_wait_bound_s_Cdly0 = 4.809 (first of 5 rows at this time)") (5 rows); [docs/notes/stomp-speed.md](../../docs/notes/stomp-speed.md "HX Stomp speed (T061, stomp-engine, 2026-10-16, firmware 3.80)")]_
- Two-path presets rendered -18 to -22 dB away from single-path renders. Cause: the compiler kept the single-path base's junctions (Balance/Pan 0/1, attach points 0/0, topology code 0), so the pedal re-derived a different layout. Fixed by writing HX Edit's layout; now at the editor preset's own repeat floor. _[ledger [2026-10-16T01:06:38+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L304 "parity · stomp · stomp-two-path · null_vs_hxedit_db = -9.3 (first of 4 rows at this time)") (4 rows); [DECISIONS 2026-10-16 stomp-engine T063](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-16-stomp-engine-t063-two-path-layout-for-the-lead-benchmark-and-search "2026-10-16 (stomp-engine, T063 two-path layout; for the lead, benchmark and search)")]_

**Decisions**

- macOS control route: USB interface 0 via stompd behind the JSON-lines protocol ([DECISIONS 2026-10-14 stomp-engine](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-stomp-engine-t018s-rendered-for-the-lead "2026-10-14 (stomp-engine, T018s rendered; for the lead)"))
- Edit buffer only, no flash writes (same entry)
- Block values win in every generated preset; every load is verified by readback ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 T051 and snapshot values](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-native-engine-t051-snapshot-values-in-native "2026-10-15 (native-engine, T051 snapshot values in Native)"))
- Firmware 3.80 is the pedal's firmware since 2026-10-15; everything measured on 3.50 is rechecked as it is used ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 Stomp firmware upgrade](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-stomp-firmware-upgrade-human "2026-10-15 (Stomp firmware upgrade, human)"))
- reamp_capture --user-di only reads the pedal ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 user-DI capture](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-user-di-capture-lead "2026-10-15 (user-DI capture, lead)"))
- Hum and Ripple frozen at 0 on both engines (DECISIONS 2026-10-16 human)
- The dual-path-only comparison rule is lifted: two-path passes may be used for checks ([DECISIONS 2026-10-16 stomp-engine, T063 two-path layout, lead](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-16-stomp-engine-t063-two-path-layout-for-the-lead-benchmark-and-search "2026-10-16 (stomp-engine, T063 two-path layout; for the lead, benchmark and search)"))

**Risks**

- Pedal control unavailable on Windows (macOS confirmed, Windows unverified)
- Native and Stomp differ in cab Early Reflections (parity)
- Stomp results not reproducible by others while stompd is unpublished
- Unexplained delay readback values on the pedal

#### Engine interface and selection

**Status:** working · **Health:** ok · **Evidence:** unit-tests, hardware

**Purpose.** One interface (load, set_params, render, cost, capabilities, close) so every other step is engine-agnostic, plus the rule for which engine runs.  
**Done when.** The interface is frozen, both engines implement it, and the auto selection (Native searches, Stomp verifies) runs a real search and verification pass in one session.  
**Functionality today.** Both engines implement the frozen interface; TONEMATCH_ENGINE=native\|stomp\|auto selects one; engine-smoke detects an engine, renders a clip and checks level, silence, NaN and length.  
**Progress note.** The contract is frozen (2026-10-14) and implemented twice, with a registry and a smoke script. render_many was added as an optional method on 2026-10-16 (contract amendment; Native pool and Stomp two-path implementations), and the auto role split has not been exercised by a search-then-verify run.  
**Board tasks.** 3 of 3 done: [T001](../../board/TASKS.md#t001 "Freeze engine interface v0"), [T008](../../board/TASKS.md#t008 "engine-smoke script and the first ledger row"), [T015](../../board/TASKS.md#t015 "Re-run engine-smoke on each engine once one is present")  
**Owners.** tone-matcher-lead, native-engine, stomp-engine, benchmark

#### Preset generation and validation (.hlx, catalog import)

**Status:** working · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, hardware

**Purpose.** Represent, write and validate a Line 6 .hlx preset that HX Edit accepts and the pedal loads, using a model catalog read at runtime from the user's own install.  
**Done when.** Search results become a valid .hlx (full-rig and effects-only, up to three snapshots, DSP budget and amp/cab pairing respected) that imports into HX Edit and loads on the pedal, for every block the search can choose.  
**Functionality today.** Reads and writes .hlx byte-equal for both golden templates, imports the 677-model catalog from HX Edit 3.82, validates blocks, DSP cost, pairing and device version, and exports full-rig or effects-only; does not yet build snapshots from a match. Generated files are not yet forced to carry snapshot values equal to their blocks (the check lives in the parity script and the engines). 2026-10-17 ([T109](../../board/TASKS.md#t109 "Curated corpus analysis")): the curated corpus (2,068 presets after cleaning) was analysed for category order, super-groups, usage concentration and metadata strength; only category-level statistics are committed.  
**Progress note.** Parse, byte-equal round trip, catalog import, validation and both export modes exist and one generated file was accepted by HX Edit and the pedal. Snapshot generation from a search result, the schema extensions, and the DSP unit and budget semantics are unverified or not built. [T101](../../board/TASKS.md#t101 "Chain-pattern model for the chain builder") (2026-10-17): chain-pattern prior built from 129 presets (aggregate statistics only, data/chain_prior/category_prior.json).  
**Board tasks.** 5 of 5 done: [T005](../../board/TASKS.md#t005 "catalog-import: locate the user's HX Edit install on each OS, read…"), [T006](../../board/TASKS.md#t006 ".hlx round-trip: parse a golden template into Preset, write it back…"), [T010](../../board/TASKS.md#t010 "Confirm Stomp firmware 3.80 and Native 3.82"), [T101](../../board/TASKS.md#t101 "Chain-pattern model for the chain builder"), [T109](../../board/TASKS.md#t109 "Curated corpus analysis")  
**Owners.** preset

**Challenges and findings**

- The Stomp golden says device_version 3.50 and the Native golden 3.80; generated files must match the pedal's firmware or HX Edit refuses them (error -8309 means wrong device). _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 cycle 2](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-cycle-2-mode-1 "2026-10-14 (cycle 2, mode 1)"); [Gate 1](../../board/GATES.md#gate-1--both-engines-driven-by-code-end-of-week-1 "Gate 1 — both engines driven by code (end of week 1)") prep]_
- known.hlx has been accepted, but only one generated file has ever gone through HX Edit and the pedal; the two ledger rows are human-reported, not observed by benchmark. _[[board/GATES.md](../../board/GATES.md "Gates") [Gate 1](../../board/GATES.md#gate-1--both-engines-driven-by-code-end-of-week-1 "Gate 1 — both engines driven by code (end of week 1)")]_
- Slots follow real @position order, not a fixed 8-slot template; the catalog's per-model load seeds a DSP cost table whose units and budget are unverified. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 cycle 2](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-cycle-2-mode-1 "2026-10-14 (cycle 2, mode 1)")]_
- A file whose snapshots disagree with its blocks plays the snapshot values on the pedal but the block values in Native; one earlier generated file had this and a live session heard the difference. parity_null.py check now refuses such files, but the preset writers themselves do not yet enforce the rule. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-15 snapshot values](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-native-engine-t051-snapshot-values-in-native "2026-10-15 (native-engine, T051 snapshot values in Native)"); ledger [2026-10-15T19:42:45](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L145 "parity · native · smoke · snapshot_stale_vs_block_db = -330.1 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)]_

#### Guitar separation and section segmentation

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, synthetic, real-songs

**Purpose.** Isolate the guitar from a mix, say how reliable the isolation is, and cluster the song into at most three tone groups for snapshots.  
**Done when.** On a stress set of real songs the separator's guitar stem is good enough that the loss still ranks the true tone first (a curve of match quality against separation quality), the confidence estimate is calibrated, and segmentation agrees with a human's section labels.  
**Functionality today.** Separates a song with htdemucs_6s (7.8 s warm on MPS, 13.1 s on CPU for 32 s), accepts user-supplied stems, segments into sections and at most three tone groups, and computes a heuristic confidence mask; nothing here is shown to work on a real recording.  
**Progress note.** htdemucs_6s runs on MPS and CPU and the pieces exist, but the only quality number is an SDR of 0.002 dB on a synthetic Karplus-Strong mix where the guitar went to the wrong stem, so real-song quality is unmeasured. Confidence is an uncalibrated heuristic, segmentation is unvalidated, and the RoFormer path was never run. On the first real song the htdemucs_6s guitar stem and its per-sample confidence (0.994 on the chosen window) were used for the target; no ground truth exists, so quality remains unmeasured.  
**Board tasks.** 3 of 3 done: [T020](../../board/TASKS.md#t020 "separate skill with backend detection and htdemucs_6s first"), [T020b](../../board/TASKS.md#t020b "RoFormer adapter licence read + install"), [T070](../../board/TASKS.md#t070 "Separation stress curve sep-stress-v1")  
**Owners.** separation

**Implemented**

- separate() with backend detection (CUDA, MPS, ROCm, DirectML, CPU) and a cache keyed by song hash [`src/tonematch/separation/core.py`](../../src/tonematch/separation/core.py "separate() and recombine_and_reseparate().") [`src/tonematch/separation/models.py`](../../src/tonematch/separation/models.py "Pluggable separators. `htdemucs_6s` is the default; RoFormer checkpoints sit behind a flag.") [`src/tonematch/separation/stems.py`](../../src/tonematch/separation/stems.py "Stems container and the on-disk cache layout (cache/stems/<song-hash>/<model>/).") [`scripts/separate.py`](../../scripts/separate.py "separate (owner: separation). See .claude/skills/separate/SKILL.md.") - Section segmentation, tone clustering to at most 3 groups, confidence mask, recombine-and-re-separate comparison [`src/tonematch/separation/segment.py`](../../src/tonematch/separation/segment.py "Beats, bars, sections and snapshot grouping (first cut; constant-tempo, 4/4 assumed).") **Challenges and findings**

- On the synthetic mix htdemucs_6s put the guitar into the 'other' stem (SDR about 0 dB), so a clean-synthetic test says nothing about real songs. _[ledger [2026-10-14T18:20:57+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L32 "separate · - · synthetic-sep-v0 · guitar_sdr_db = 0.002")]_
- The only guitar-capable catalogue checkpoint (BS-Roformer-SW) has no stated licence and an unverifiable origin; it is gated behind TONEMATCH_ROFORMER_LICENCE_ACK=1 and nothing was downloaded. mel-roformer and mdx23c have no guitar-capable checkpoint. _[[docs/notes/separation-models.md](../../docs/notes/separation-models.md "Separation models, weights and licences (separation agent, T020)"); DECISIONS separation entries]_
- Optional extra diffq is CC BY-NC 4.0: blocks any bundled or commercial release; soxr is LGPL-2.1 and accepted as a WARN. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 RoFormer licence rulings](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-cycle-3-roformer-extra-licence-rulings-lead "2026-10-14 (cycle 3, RoFormer extra licence rulings, lead)")]_
- The confidence estimate (dominance times consistency) is uncalibrated and read 0.044 on the synthetic mix. _[ledger [2026-10-14T18:19:39+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L26 "separate · - · synthetic-sep-v0 · wall_clock_s = 7.793 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)]_

- Calibrate the confidence estimate against measured quality
- Validate segmentation on real songs
- Try a RoFormer checkpoint if a licensed one is found

**Open blockers**

- No real-song stress set and the only guitar-capable RoFormer checkpoint has no stated licence; the stress curve so far is on a synthetic mix.

**Decisions**

- User-supplied stems accepted; hosted separation deferred ([DECISIONS 2026-10-13 planning](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-13-planning-before-the-repo "2026-10-13 (planning, before the repo)"))
- RoFormer opt-in and ungranted; default htdemucs_6s ([DECISIONS 2026-10-14 RoFormer rulings](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-cycle-3-roformer-extra-licence-rulings-lead "2026-10-14 (cycle 3, RoFormer extra licence rulings, lead)"))
- GPU optional and not NVIDIA-specific ([DECISIONS 2026-10-13 planning](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-13-planning-before-the-repo "2026-10-13 (planning, before the repo)"))

**Risks**

- Separation artifacts mislead the loss: high (PLAN risk table)
- Several guitars in one stem: medium

**Key files.** [`src/tonematch/separation/core.py`](../../src/tonematch/separation/core.py "separate() and recombine_and_reseparate().") · [`src/tonematch/separation/segment.py`](../../src/tonematch/separation/segment.py "Beats, bars, sections and snapshot grouping (first cut; constant-tempo, 4/4 assumed).") · [`docs/notes/separation-models.md`](../../docs/notes/separation-models.md "Separation models, weights and licences (separation agent, T020)") · [`tests/test_separation.py`](../../tests/test_separation.py "Separation tests: short synthetic audio only; no torch, no model weights, no network.")

#### Reference DI library (dry signal)

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, real-songs, ear (open listening, one negative blind session)

**Purpose.** Supply a clean DI clip that matches a tone's register and style, because the engines need dry input and the song has none.  
**Done when.** A licence-clean DI library is indexed locally, the picker returns clips that make the loss rank the true preset first more often than a single fixed clip does, and the user's own DI is a working accuracy mode.  
**Functionality today.** Indexes a folder of DI clips by register and style and picks one; the local Guitar-TECHS set is indexed and the picker chose the DI for the DS-Sample match. The user-DI play-along capture (reamp-capture) records the user's own guitar dry and through the pedal in one stream; a hardware take of it appears to exist (see discrepancies).  
**Progress note.** 2026-10-22: Basic Pitch (Apache-2.0, local venv) transcribes the separated guitar stems; a dry guitar is rebuilt from the notes using real Guitar-TECHS notes or the human's own calibrated notes ([T201](../../board/TASKS.md#t201 "Rebuild a dry DI track for Mother from the Basic Pitch transcription…"), [T203](../../board/TASKS.md#t203 "Install Spotify Basic Pitch locally")); an optional chord-list rebuild exists ([T205](../../board/TASKS.md#t205 "rebuild_dry --chords")); the human recorded a guided calibration session and the profile prs-7-tone (263 own notes, tone curve, level -36.1 dBFS) was built ([T204](../../board/TASKS.md#t204 "Guitar calibration (human 2026-10-22)")). The rebuild is approximate (alignment 0.29-0.46 on slow parts, unmeasurable on dense riffs). The level convention is undecided. Picker benefit vs a fixed clip is still unmeasured. Correction 2026-10-23: the rebuilt dry's own contribution is unmeasured; the ablation 'same search, generic library DI' was never run, and the chord result (better dry, worse match by the measure, plain preferred by ear) suggests the rebuild acts as a level-and-rhythm proxy, not a timbre proxy ([docs/notes/research-dry-signal-2026-10-23.md](../../docs/notes/research-dry-signal-2026-10-23.md "Dry-signal strategy: is the concatenative rebuild sound, and what else is there? (T219)")). The [T211](../../board/TASKS.md#t211 "All seven songs with the calibrated guitar") calibrated re-run has no ear verdict and its distances are across different dry tracks. 2026-10-25 ([T224](../../board/TASKS.md#t224 "Dry-signal ablation (decision 4) + level-only ablation")): the dry-signal ablation ran: four dry arms per song plus a level-only arm, 30 searches, 10,320 renders. Against the plain rebuild, the stem-envelope arm kept the same drive model on 6 of 7 songs, the library-DI arm and the level-only arm on 5 of 7; DS-Sample changed model in every arm and Satisfaction's plain pick is a dead tie. Distances are not comparable across arms and no fidelity claim is made. Packs are built; the ear has not rated them. Level convention: play-along level -28.1 dBFS is the default (2026-10-25 bundle, provisional).  
**Board tasks.** 8 of 8 done: [T021](../../board/TASKS.md#t021 "Reference DI library index over EGDB"), [T042](../../board/TASKS.md#t042 "Play-along user-DI capture"), [T201](../../board/TASKS.md#t201 "Rebuild a dry DI track for Mother from the Basic Pitch transcription…"), [T203](../../board/TASKS.md#t203 "Install Spotify Basic Pitch locally"), [T204](../../board/TASKS.md#t204 "Guitar calibration (human 2026-10-22)"), [T205](../../board/TASKS.md#t205 "rebuild_dry --chords"), [T224](../../board/TASKS.md#t224 "Dry-signal ablation (decision 4) + level-only ablation"), [T021b](../../board/TASKS.md#t021b "Download Guitar-TECHS DI subset into data/di/, index, real smoke clip")  
**Owners.** dry-signal

**Challenges and findings**

- DI mismatch biases EQ and gain; content-invariant losses and several reference DIs per tone are the mitigation, neither measured yet. _[PLAN risk table]_
- Licences of candidate DI datasets are not yet recorded in a model card or dataset card; licence-check flags data/ for hand review. _[reviews/licence-2026-10-14.md]_
- Preset loss is strongly DI-dependent: the same chain scores 7.7 on the picked DI and 11.0 and 22.7 on two others, so a fixed DI may bias the match. _[board T041; [DECISIONS 2026-10-14 search T041](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-search-t041-first-end-to-end-run-on-ds-sample-proposals-for-the-lead "2026-10-14 (search, T041 first end-to-end run on DS-Sample; proposals for the lead)")]_

**Still to do**

- Measure whether the picker beats a fixed clip on the synthetic set
- Hardware-test the user-DI accuracy mode and report
- Blind DI recovery and A+B hybrid are research only

**Open blockers**

- The picker's benefit is unmeasured; the user-DI accuracy mode needs a hardware test with the human's guitar.

**Decisions**

- Dry signal v0: B (reference DI library) default, E (blind estimation) as starting guess ([DECISIONS 2026-10-13 planning](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-13-planning-before-the-repo "2026-10-13 (planning, before the repo)"))

**Risks**

- DI mismatch biases EQ and gain: medium (PLAN risk table)

#### Loss kit (tone metrics)

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, proxy, synthetic, ear (open listening, one negative blind session)

**Purpose.** Score how close a candidate render is to the target tone, with terms that are trustworthy despite separation artifacts and DI mismatch.  
**Done when.** At least one loss term, or a combination, passes the loss-trust-check threshold on the Helix synthetic set (true preset ranked first against distractors), so it has non-zero default weights, and the ear agrees with the metric on real songs.  
**Functionality today.** LossKit.score() returns per-term scores (LTAS tilt, crest/flatness/HNR, envelope, multi-resolution STFT) but with default weights 0 its objective is flat; the search must pass provisional weights explicitly and label results unadmitted. Update 2026-10-17: a second multi-resolution spectral term (mrstft_v2, version 2.0) is admitted alone, for the known-DI mode only, at weight 1.0; the default and provisional weights are unchanged.  
**Progress note.** Four no-DI terms and a LossKit exist, but none is admitted (all default weights 0) and the only trust measurement is on non-Helix proxy signals. Envelope v1 ([T025](../../board/TASKS.md#t025 "Sharpen envelope (weakest proxy term, held-out rank-1 0.05)")) improved the weakest term but held-out rank-1 on fresh seeds is only 0.117 against chance 0.0625 and 15 distractors; learned embeddings were researched, not run. AFx-Rep (ST-ITO) was wired in as an unadmitted embedding term and was worse than the DSP terms on proxy signals. On the first real song the loss ranked chain K first by 0.015 over H, while the human informally preferred H; the three level-invariant terms also accepted an output 52 dB too quiet until a guard was added in match_song.py. 2026-10-16: the first trust check on real Helix Native renders (helix-trust-v1: 40 known chains, 19 look-alikes each, 2,400 renders) admitted no term. On new notes of the same recording the best term managed rank-1 0.175 (7 of 40; chance 0.05), against a proposed bar of 0.90. With the same notes and the separation stand-in, mrstft reached 0.725 (0.875 against audible look-alikes only). The terms move more with the notes played than with the tone; reweighting did not help (0.15). No weights admitted (ledger [2026-10-16T15:50:17+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L597 "loss-trust · native · helix-trust-v1 · rank1_rate = 0.025")). [T096](../../board/TASKS.md#t096 "Gate 2 option A + rule B") (2026-10-17): mrstft_v2 2.0 was tuned on the train split, frozen, and scored once on the test split (0.990 against 0.90), admitted for the known-DI mode by the lead. It has not been judged by ear and has not been used on real songs. Correction 2026-10-23: the admission was the lead's and is unapproved. On 2026-10-18 the human judged the model built on it unusable ('the model we created is garbage') and declared every lead-made method ruling, including the admitted weights, unapproved until reviewed ([DECISIONS 2026-10-18 model and method decisions](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-18-human-recorded-by-lead-model-and-method-decisions-require-the-humans-approval-the-current-model-is-judged-unusable "2026-10-18 (human, recorded by lead): model and method decisions require the human's approval; the current model is judged unusable")). In blind rating v3 on hard same-performance pairs mrstft_v2 agreed with the ear 10 of 25 times (chance); the provisional kit 14 of 25. The de facto ranker is now the AFx-Rep embedding distance: by ear it orders models on the same performance (v3 18 of 25; v5 20 of 21, on the same experiment's own picks), is unvalidated for knob moves ([T175](../../board/TASKS.md#t175 "Different-guitar test of embed_afxrep, ltas_eqfit, ltas_tilt on drive") invariance ratio 30 for small moves) and across different dry tracks, and has never been rated against a separated stem. Status moved from working to prototype: the kit runs, but no measure is approved and none is trusted for the song case. 2026-10-26: the [T223](../../board/TASKS.md#t223 "Blind validation of AFx-Rep against separated stems") stem-validation pack (41 trials, pre-registered) is unscored; the separation-consistent re-score ([T227](../../board/TASKS.md#t227 "[stopped 2026-10-25 by the lead")) was stopped by the lead after the verdict and can resume.  
**Board tasks.** 7 of 8 done, 1 in progress: [T022](../../board/TASKS.md#t022 "Loss kit v0 with the no-DI terms"), [T025](../../board/TASKS.md#t025 "Sharpen envelope (weakest proxy term, held-out rank-1 0.05)"), [T032](../../board/TASKS.md#t032 "Wire AFx-Rep (ST-ITO) in as loss term embed_afxrep"), [T066](../../board/TASKS.md#t066 "Parity metrics for time-based effects"), [T067](../../board/TASKS.md#t067 "Gate 2 priority 1: loss-trust-check on real Helix Native renders"), [T096](../../board/TASKS.md#t096 "Gate 2 option A + rule B"), [T221](../../board/TASKS.md#t221 "DI-invariance ratio check"), [T223](../../board/TASKS.md#t223 "Blind validation of AFx-Rep against separated stems")

**Challenges and findings**

- No loss term is admitted. Against random distractors the provisional sum ranked the truth first 0.95 of the time, but against near distractors (one parameter group re-drawn) held-out rank-1 was 0.225 at best; easy distractors overstate the kit. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 cycle 3 loss kit](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-cycle-3-loss-kit-lead "2026-10-14 (cycle 3, loss kit, lead)")]_
- mrstft is only valid with an aligned DI (0.775 same-notes, 0.00 held-out), so it cannot be used on a song with an unknown DI. _[ledger [2026-10-14T18:27:57+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L33 "loss-trust · - · proxy-dsp-v0 · rank1_rate = 0.225 (first of 8 rows at this time)") (8 rows)]_
- Envelope is the weakest term. v1 (spectral-flux onsets, sub-ms attack, per-note decay, sustain slope, noise floor) lifted held-out rank-1 from 0.033 to 0.117 on fresh seeds, still weak; same-notes 0.15 to 0.325 mostly reflects knowing the notes. Crest after the cab barely tracks drive; spectral flatness carries that term. _[board T025; ledger [2026-10-14T21:17:05](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L52 "loss-trust · - · proxy-dsp-v0 · rank1_rate = 0.0333 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)..21:20:25+00:00]_
- The confidence mask had no effect on the proxy because the proxy separator introduced no artifacts; its value is untested. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 tone-metrics](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-tone-metrics-t032-afx-rep-as-embed_afxrep "2026-10-14 (tone-metrics, T032 AFx-Rep as embed_afxrep)") entry]_
- Only AFx-Rep is cleanly licensed (Apache-2.0, 1.16 GB checkpoint); Fx-Encoder++ is CC BY-NC 4.0 and Open-Amp has no licence file. No weights downloaded. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 cycle 3 loss kit](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-cycle-3-loss-kit-lead "2026-10-14 (cycle 3, loss kit, lead)")]_
- AFx-Rep embedding term (embed_afxrep, weight 0): held-out rank-1 0.067 versus 0.133 for ltas_tilt and 0.150 for envelope on fresh proxy seeds, so worse and not admitted; 0.065 s per 10 s clip on CPU. _[board T032; ledger [2026-10-14T23:14:41](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L56 "loss-trust · - · proxy-dsp-v0 · rank1_rate = 0.0667 (first of 6 rows at this time)") (6 rows) loss-trust]_
- The loss does not agree with the human on the one real song: it ranked chain K first by 0.015, the human preferred H. One song, informal, not blind, so this is a warning and not a measurement. _[ledger [2026-10-15T19:10:17](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L132 "ab · native · DS-Sample · ab_pref_rank = 2.0") human ab]_
- The loss terms are level-invariant, so a 52 dB quieter output scored better; match_song.py adds a level guard that is not an admitted term. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 search T041](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-search-t041-first-end-to-end-run-on-ds-sample-proposals-for-the-lead "2026-10-14 (search, T041 first end-to-end run on DS-Sample; proposals for the lead)")]_
- Every 'closer' claim from 2026-10-22 on compares distances between renders of different dry tracks (other notes, tone, level), which [T041](../../board/TASKS.md#t041 "End-to-end on a real song") (7.7 / 11.0 / 22.7 for one preset on three DIs) and [T175](../../board/TASKS.md#t175 "Different-guitar test of embed_afxrep, ltas_eqfit, ltas_tilt on drive") show the measures cannot order. No ear verdict exists for those comparisons. _[[docs/notes/methodology-audit-2026-10-23.md](../../docs/notes/methodology-audit-2026-10-23.md "Methodology audit, 2026-10-23 (independent, read-only)") section 2]_

**Still to do**

- Propose admission for the separated-song case with drive as the named weak category
- Option C: note-proof terms for the no-DI case ([Gate 3](../../board/GATES.md#gate-3--blind-prediction-of-a-single-block-without-the-dry-track-new-human-2026-10-18 "Gate 3 — blind prediction of a single block without the dry track (new, human 2026-10-18)"))
- Option D: learned guitar embedding (deferred)
- Freeze [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") thresholds with benchmark

**Open blockers**

- No measure is approved by the human. AFx-Rep orders the final candidates as an interim default (provisional, 2026-10-25 bundle) and its blind stem validation was answered with a verdict ('so bad I can't even compare them'), not a scored ranking, so agreement with the ear on separated guitars is still unmeasured. mrstft_v2 stays unapproved and at chance on hard same-performance pairs.

**Decisions**

- No loss term is admitted; search must pass PROVISIONAL_WEIGHTS and label results ([DECISIONS 2026-10-14 cycle 3 loss kit](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-cycle-3-loss-kit-lead "2026-10-14 (cycle 3, loss kit, lead)"))
- Dataset name proxy-dsp-v0 kept out of synthetic-v&lt;N&gt; comparisons (same entry)
- Layered comparison ([DECISIONS 2026-10-13 planning](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-13-planning-before-the-repo "2026-10-13 (planning, before the repo)"))

**Risks**

- Separation artifacts mislead the loss: high (PLAN risk table)
- DI mismatch biases EQ and gain: medium

#### Block probe and fingerprints

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, proxy

**Purpose.** Characterise each Helix block on its own (frequency response, harmonic and clipping curve, intermodulation, dynamics, delay, reverb and modulation) with a fixed probe signal, so search can choose models per slot from a fingerprint library and the Native-versus-Stomp audit can find mismatches like Early Reflections block by block.  
**Done when.** The probe signal is rendered through any single block at a grid of settings in Native (and optionally the Stomp), fingerprints are stored locally, compare tolerances are set from measured repeat spread and parity blocks, the per-block parity audit has run, and search uses the fingerprints to seed model choice.  
**Functionality today.** Builds the probe signal and layout, analyses a recorded response into a fingerprint and compares two fingerprints. Works on synthetic blocks only (non-Helix stand-ins); no Helix block has been probed.  
**Progress note.** The offline half is done and validated: the probe set, analysis and fingerprint format exist and 63 of 63 checks pass against known numpy and scipy blocks. Nothing has been run through Native or the Stomp, so no Helix block has a fingerprint, the compare tolerances are provisional, and the probe is 47 s against a roughly 30 s per model budget.  
**Board tasks.** 4 of 4 done: [T046](../../board/TASKS.md#t046 "block-probe skill, offline part"), [T047](../../board/TASKS.md#t047 "started 2026-10-16 (native-engine, engine push N2)"), [T048](../../board/TASKS.md#t048 "[breadth first, category order"), [T049](../../board/TASKS.md#t049 "Per-block parity audit on the Stomp using the probe set")  
**Owners.** tone-metrics, native-engine, benchmark

**Implemented**

- Probe signal, analysis, fingerprint schema (v1) and synthetic validation blocks [`src/tonematch/probe/signals.py`](../../src/tonematch/probe/signals.py "The block-probe signal set, version `probe-v1` (T046).") [`src/tonematch/probe/analysis.py`](../../src/tonematch/probe/analysis.py "Analyse a block's render of the probe set into a fingerprint (schema in docs/notes/block-probe.md).") [`src/tonematch/probe/fingerprint.py`](../../src/tonematch/probe/fingerprint.py "Block fingerprint format (schema `tonematch.block-fingerprint` v1) and the per-feature compare.") [`src/tonematch/probe/synthetic.py`](../../src/tonematch/probe/synthetic.py "Known synthetic blocks (numpy/scipy) for validating the block-probe analysis. NOT Helix models.")
- Command line: make, analyse, compare, validate [`scripts/block_probe.py`](../../scripts/block_probe.py "block-probe, offline part (owner: tone-metrics, T046). Skill: .claude/skills/block-probe/SKILL.md.") [`tests/test_probe.py`](../../tests/test_probe.py "block-probe offline part (T046): probe set, analysis against KNOWN synthetic blocks, fingerprint")

**Challenges and findings**

- Modulation is measured on a sustained carrier, not the impulse, because one impulse cannot show an LFO rate; the probe is 47 s (38 s without the DI excerpts), above the roughly 30 s per model assumed for the overnight capture, so a shorter variant may be needed. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-15 block-probe offline part](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-block-probe-offline-part-tone-metrics-t046 "2026-10-15 (block-probe offline part, tone-metrics, T046)")]_
- Compare tolerances are provisional until the Native repeat-render spread and a Stomp-versus-Native spread on blocks known to be in parity are measured. All validation so far is on non-Helix stand-ins and says nothing about how Helix blocks behave. _[[docs/notes/block-probe.md](../../docs/notes/block-probe.md "block-probe (T046, offline part)"); ledger [2026-10-15T19:31:39](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L141 "smoke · - · probe-synth-v1 · probe_checks_pass_rate = 1.0")]_

- [T048](../../board/TASKS.md#t048 "[breadth first, category order"): overnight capture of all Stomp-compatible models, mono blocks first
- [T049](../../board/TASKS.md#t049 "Per-block parity audit on the Stomp using the probe set"): set tolerances and run the per-block parity audit on the Stomp
- Cut a shorter probe if render time requires it
- Add a ledger kind 'probe' (proposal to benchmark)

**Open blockers**

- [T047](../../board/TASKS.md#t047 "started 2026-10-16 (native-engine, engine push N2)") (rendering the probe through Native) is in progress; the block library it needs ([T048](../../board/TASKS.md#t048 "[breadth first, category order")) is captured (ledger [2026-10-16T15:25:12+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L473 "render-cost · native · block-library-v1 · capture_per_chain_s = 0.563 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)); a stereo-capable host is still open.

**Decisions**

- Blocks are characterised individually, matches are judged on the whole chain ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 per-block approach, human-approved](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-per-block-approach-human-approved "2026-10-15 (per-block approach, human-approved)"))
- Fingerprint schema v1 and provisional tolerances ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 block-probe offline part](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-block-probe-offline-part-tone-metrics-t046 "2026-10-15 (block-probe offline part, tone-metrics, T046)"))

**Risks**

- Block-serial accuracy has an unexplained floor near -67 dB once an amp follows other blocks
- Stereo blocks and dual cabs cannot be probed until the Native host supports them

#### Search and optimiser

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, synthetic, hardware, real-songs, ear (open listening, one negative blind session)

**Purpose.** Find the preset whose render best matches the target section within a render budget set by the engine's cost.  
**Done when.** On the Helix synthetic-v1 set the search recovers known presets at the thresholds benchmark set (block-type F1, model accuracy, parameter MAE), then beats the nearest community preset on real songs, with the final candidate verified on the pedal.  
**Functionality today.** match() plans a render budget, searches a chain and its parameters with CMA-ES against an unadmitted loss, applies a level guard and a weak prior, and writes a report and listening pack. match_song.py ran it on one real song: best provisional loss 7.71 (raw DI 24.93). Renders now cost about 0.28 s, so earlier budgets were over-planned. Recovery of known answers is unproven on real engine renders. Current method: broad draws over 47 pool models, top-K narrowing, successive halving with banded CMA-ES within 300 renders, with measured seeding of delay, reverb and modulation settings.  
**Progress note.** CMA-ES over a captured template, beam over chains, budget planning, a level guard and a report writer are built, and one real-song run completed end to end on Native (449 renders, 1506 s; provisional loss 13.79 to 7.71, unadmitted). But the loss it optimises is not trusted (the human preferred the runner-up), recovery of known answers is shown only on a fake engine, there is no Helix known-answer run, no predictor or retrieval seeds, no model choice per slot, and no successive halving on hardware. The search now uses the Stomp speed options by default ([T062](../../board/TASKS.md#t062 "Wire the T061 Stomp speed options into scripts/match_song.py as the…"): 3 s search clips, readback every 10 loads, measured tails, one engine per batch; a legacy flag falls back), tested with a fake engine only. The trial time on the pedal was measured as a batch (10.9 s to 3.8 s, ledger [2026-10-16T00:17:11+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L274 "render-cost · stomp · stomp-speed · search_batch_per_trial_s_before = 10.861 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows)), but the search itself has not been timed on the pedal, so there is no ledger row for its speed. 2026-10-16: the clip-length study ([T065](../../board/TASKS.md#t065 "Clip-length study on Native")) kept the full-clip default (medians 8.36 full against 9.16-11.95 for shorter policies; ledger [2026-10-16T15:24:06+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L470 "search · native · ds-sample-intro-cliplen · final_score_median_full_15seeds = 8.49 (first of 3 rows at this time)") (3 rows)). [T074](../../board/TASKS.md#t074 "N1 free every numeric CC-mapped, verified param over its template…") (written, not committed, no render run): frees every CC-verified numeric parameter over its template range, with a range-scaled prior, and makes time-variant blocks opt-in. 2026-10-16/17: the known-DI level guard ([T096b](../../board/TASKS.md#t096b "Ruling 3: known-DI level guard references the target level")) is coded; model choice per slot ([T050](../../board/TASKS.md#t050 "Model choice per slot from the block library, fingerprint-informed…")) is built and, on a 16-item development set with the old provisional score, tied the old pool pass at 7 of 16 models (DECISIONS 2026-10-16; no ledger row). The re-run with the admitted score was interrupted. 2026-10-17: measured seeding ([T113](../../board/TASKS.md#t113 "Gate 2 step, raise single-block model accuracy")) and the hybrid method ([T114](../../board/TASKS.md#t114 "Gate 2 step: --hybrid"), chosen by a rule fixed before the last run) are built. [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 1 on all 96 items did not pass: it met four of six criteria and failed category accuracy (0.802 against 0.85) and model non-inferiority to the random walk. Weakest families: modulation, reverb, amp. The next attempt uses a fresh synthetic-v2 and per-family specialists. A chain builder ([T105](../../board/TASKS.md#t105 "Chain builder (human 2026-10-17 \"build a chain from the analysis\")")) exists with fake engines only. 2026-10-20 to 23: a predictor-first drive matcher ([T196](../../board/TASKS.md#t196 "Predictor-first drive matcher option")), blind matches of five songs ([T200](../../board/TASKS.md#t200 "Blind single-drive matches of five songs the human added to data/songs")), and for seven songs a full DI-based drive search over all 60 drive models on a rebuilt dry guitar ([T207](../../board/TASKS.md#t207 "All seven songs (human 2026-10-22 \"render the dry tracks for all of…")), a knob fine-tune ([T209](../../board/TASKS.md#t209 "Knob fine-tuning on the seven T207 picks"); distance lower on 6 of 7) and a calibrated-guitar re-run ([T211](../../board/TASKS.md#t211 "All seven songs with the calibrated guitar"); lower on 5 of 7). The human heard the seven as 'pretty good' and the fine-tuned as 'the same or better' (open listening). One drive block per song; no chain. Corrections 2026-10-23 (audit section 6): the seven-song verdicts are one open pass by one listener with no alternative heard; the [T209](../../board/TASKS.md#t209 "Knob fine-tuning on the seven T207 picks") 'lower on 6 of 7' and [T211](../../board/TASKS.md#t211 "All seven songs with the calibrated guitar") 'lower on 5 of 7' figures are AFx-Rep distances across different dry tracks with no ear verdict; the [T206](../../board/TASKS.md#t206 "Re-run predictor-first drive match on Mother") 'unadmitted loss 9.53 vs 16.00' is a provisional-loss comparison across different DIs, not a fidelity comparison. Measured-pedal mode ([T187](../../board/TASKS.md#t187 "Measured-pedal mode preparation"), [docs/notes/measured-pedal-mode.md](../../docs/notes/measured-pedal-mode.md "Measured-pedal mode: the capture tool and a simulation of the real-world nuisances (T187)")) is built and simulated only (model 0.96 in simulation); it has never been run on a pedal. AFx-Rep final ranking and knob fine-tuning with Output/Master free are defaults of [scripts/song_match.py](../../scripts/song_match.py "T217: one command from a song to a drive-block preset for the guitar, with a local listening page. LOCAL ONLY.") by reuse, without an itemised approval (approved for shortlisting only on 2026-10-18); they are to be read as test settings until the human rules (decisions 1 and 3). 2026-10-26: the human's blind session on the top-5 drive candidates against the separated guitar (five songs) found them 'so bad I can't even compare them'; this is the first blind evidence on real songs and it is negative for drive-only matches. Defaults were pinned to data/approved-methods.json ([T226](../../board/TASKS.md#t226 "Governance mechanics")); knob fine-tuning is an option with Output/Master locked; the provisional loss and the drive families are no longer defaults.  
**Board tasks.** 20 of 22 done, 1 in progress: [T023](../../board/TASKS.md#t023 "CMA-ES over the fixed template using cost() for the budget"), [T041](../../board/TASKS.md#t041 "End-to-end on a real song"), [T050](../../board/TASKS.md#t050 "Model choice per slot from the block library, fingerprint-informed…"), [T062](../../board/TASKS.md#t062 "Wire the T061 Stomp speed options into scripts/match_song.py as the…"), [T065](../../board/TASKS.md#t065 "Clip-length study on Native"), [T074](../../board/TASKS.md#t074 "N1 free every numeric CC-mapped, verified param over its template…"), [T105](../../board/TASKS.md#t105 "Chain builder (human 2026-10-17 \"build a chain from the analysis\")"), [T113](../../board/TASKS.md#t113 "Gate 2 step, raise single-block model accuracy"), [T114](../../board/TASKS.md#t114 "Gate 2 step: --hybrid"), [T122](../../board/TASKS.md#t122 "Family dispatch and time-based specialist"), [T134](../../board/TASKS.md#t134 "Specialist adapters (blocking --specialists all for Gate 2 attempt 2)"), [T140](../../board/TASKS.md#t140 "Linear specialist wired into --specialists all\|linear"), [T187](../../board/TASKS.md#t187 "Measured-pedal mode preparation"), [T196](../../board/TASKS.md#t196 "Predictor-first drive matcher option"), [T200](../../board/TASKS.md#t200 "Blind single-drive matches of five songs the human added to data/songs"), [T206](../../board/TASKS.md#t206 "Re-run predictor-first drive match on Mother"), [T207](../../board/TASKS.md#t207 "All seven songs (human 2026-10-22 \"render the dry tracks for all of…"), [T209](../../board/TASKS.md#t209 "Knob fine-tuning on the seven T207 picks"), [T211](../../board/TASKS.md#t211 "All seven songs with the calibrated guitar"), [T228](../../board/TASKS.md#t228 "Colour pilot (human 2026-10-25 \"focus on our distortion model… drive…"), [T229](../../board/TASKS.md#t229 "Colour comparison on seven songs"), [T096b](../../board/TASKS.md#t096b "Ruling 3: known-DI level guard references the target level")

**Challenges and findings**

- With default LossKit weights of 0 the objective is flat, so search must be given PROVISIONAL_WEIGHTS and its output labelled unadmitted. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 cycle 3 loss kit](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-cycle-3-loss-kit-lead "2026-10-14 (cycle 3, loss kit, lead)")]_
- Budget.clip_s is 2.0 s today while loss statistics need 10 s or more; the budget needs review. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 cycle 3 loss kit](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-cycle-3-loss-kit-lead "2026-10-14 (cycle 3, loss kit, lead)")]_
- Native varies only 16 knobs and 16 switches inside a captured structure, so model choice cannot be searched on Native without new captures; model search is realistic only on the Stomp at about 2.7 s per render plus 0.66 s per model change. _[[docs/notes/synthetic-benchmark-design.md](../../docs/notes/synthetic-benchmark-design.md "Synthetic recovery set v1: design, cost, capture request, proposed thresholds (T024)")]_
- Plan.est_wall_s divided by cost().parallelism although match() renders serially (estimated 3.9 s, took 46 s) and popsize was rounded up to the parallelism for no gain. The planner now plans with parallelism 1 and notes the unused parallelism; a CMA-ES generation through NativeRenderPool or render_many would use the 2 renders/s pool but is not built. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 native-engine T026](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-native-engine-render-time-gap-t026 "2026-10-14 (native-engine, render-time gap, T026)"); [src/tonematch/search/loop.py](../../src/tonematch/search/loop.py "The matching loop: budget planning, CMA-ES refinement per chain, successive halving, chain grouping.")]_
- The first real-song run settled on corner values (several parameters at 1.0), and the human, listening informally through the Stomp outputs, judged the runner-up chain (rank 2, H) the best frequency match. The ear decides whether corners are a tone or a loss artefact; this is one song and not blind. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 search T041](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-search-t041-first-end-to-end-run-on-ds-sample-proposals-for-the-lead "2026-10-14 (search, T041 first end-to-end run on DS-Sample; proposals for the lead)"); ledger [2026-10-15T19:10:17](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L132 "ab · native · DS-Sample · ab_pref_rank = 2.0")]_
- Without a level guard the search drove channel volume to the floor and cut the loss in half on a render that would be silent; the guard is not part of the admitted loss. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 search T041](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-search-t041-first-end-to-end-run-on-ds-sample-proposals-for-the-lead "2026-10-14 (search, T041 first end-to-end run on DS-Sample; proposals for the lead)")]_
- Per-DI sensitivity: the same winning preset scores 7.7 on the picked DI and 11.0 and 22.7 on two others. _[board [T041](../../board/TASKS.md#t041 "End-to-end on a real song")]_
- Scores before the Native host fix are history-dependent, so earlier run numbers are not exactly reproducible. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-15 host memory](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-native-engine-host-memory "2026-10-15 (native-engine, host memory)")]_
- The DS-Sample tuned chain beat the tutorial on 6 decided trials with 1 of 2 repeats consistent, on one song and one window; the tuned EQ sits at the +-12 dB limit on 8 of 10 bands, and a further tune improved the score and was heard as identical. _[[docs/notes/methodology-audit-2026-10-23.md](../../docs/notes/methodology-audit-2026-10-23.md "Methodology audit, 2026-10-23 (independent, read-only)") section 1; DECISIONS 2026-10-20]_

**Still to do**

- Re-plan budgets at about 0.3 s per render
- Predictor and retrieval seeds (needs Learning)
- Successive halving on the Stomp and a hardware final check of the top candidate
- Make cab Early Reflections fixed at 0 for anything verified on the Stomp until parity exists
- Time the search with the Stomp speed options on the pedal
- Build per-family specialists: modulation, reverb and delay, amp (DECISIONS 2026-10-17)
- Run attempt 2 on synthetic-v2 under the frozen rule

**Open blockers**

- [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 2 failed ranking the true preset first (0.9375 against 0.95); modulation and drive are weak; attempt 3 needs a fresh sealed set.

**Decisions**

- Search passes provisional weights explicitly ([DECISIONS 2026-10-14 cycle 3 loss kit](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-cycle-3-loss-kit-lead "2026-10-14 (cycle 3, loss kit, lead)"))
- Edit-buffer-only rule and cost() budgets ([DECISIONS 2026-10-13 cycle 1 and 2026-10-14 cycle](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-13-cycle-1-mode-1 "2026-10-13 (cycle 1, mode 1)") 2)
- Bound free parameters and fix cab Early Reflections at 0 for Stomp-bound runs ([DECISIONS 2026-10-15 parity finding](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-parity-finding-cab-earlyreflections-lead-on-benchmark "2026-10-15 (parity finding: cab EarlyReflections, lead on benchmark)"))

**Risks**

- Search space too large for the budget: medium (PLAN risk table)
- Hardware budget too small for a close match: medium
