#### Benchmark, ledger and gates (evaluation)

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, synthetic, hardware

**Purpose.** Measure progress with known answers, record every run in an append-only ledger, and decide when a gate is passed.  
**Done when.** The synthetic-v1 recovery set is captured and rendered, thresholds are agreed, CI runs the smoke recovery on every change, the separation stress curve and parity null are recorded, and Gates 2 and 3 are evidenced and signed.  
**Functionality today.** Appends and reads the ledger, runs engine-smoke, generates 180 labelled synthetic items (labels, no audio), and measures Stomp-versus-Native parity on six presets with a restore check. Cannot yet render the known-answer set, run a recovery, or run a blind listen.  
**Progress note.** The ledger, [Gate 1](../../board/GATES.md#gate-1--both-engines-driven-by-code-end-of-week-1 "Gate 1 — both engines driven by code (end of week 1)"), the synthetic-benchmark design and labels generator, and now a parity-null tool with a measured Stomp-versus-Native table exist. No Helix known-answer set has been rendered, no recovery has run, thresholds are approved as working targets but not frozen, no separation stress curve exists, there is no blind-ab script, and CI still skips the benchmark. 2026-10-16: parity spot checks on firmware 3.80 ([T060](../../board/TASKS.md#t060 "Parity spot checks: at least 5 models per block category on Stomp")) and time-effect parity by measurement ([T066](../../board/TASKS.md#t066 "Parity metrics for time-based effects"), [T066b](../../board/TASKS.md#t066b "Parity probe session closing the T066 open questions"), [T066c](../../board/TASKS.md#t066c "Parity method rule and the Gray Flanger / Deluxe Phaser close-out")) set where Native stands in for the pedal; the 96-item synthetic-v1 set and its runner are built ([T073](../../board/TASKS.md#t073 "Gate 2 synthetic recovery")), but only a 3-item provisional run exists (true preset first 0 of 3 on new notes, not gate evidence). [T100](../../board/TASKS.md#t100 "Ruling 2 in scripts/synthetic_v1.py") (Ruling 2, same-notes rank-1 in the known-DI mode) and the blind A/B tool ([T104](../../board/TASKS.md#t104 "Song and artist context as a prior"), benchmark) are done; Native verification ([T103](../../board/TASKS.md#t103 "Native verification suite scripts/native_verify.py")) is running. 2026-10-17: the pass rule was frozen before the full run; attempt 1 is recorded and [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") is not ticked. A blind A/B tool ([T107](../../board/TASKS.md#t107 "Blind A/B listening tool scripts/ab_session.py")) and a local explainer report ([T115](../../board/TASKS.md#t115 "Local Gate 2 report for a non-technical reader")) exist; the explainer stays off the public repository. 2026-10-25 ([T223](../../board/TASKS.md#t223 "Blind validation of AFx-Rep against separated stems")): gate3-v1 sealed (120 single drive-block items, 60 product drive models x 2, Guitar-TECHS P3, seed 223001, labels sha256 88a8880c...), bars G3-1 to G3-5 written down before the draw, scoring script refuses to run without a launch reference. Limit: the items share about 31 non-overlapping 10 s windows of playing, so they are not independent. [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") closed as superseded.  
**Board tasks.** 13 of 15 done, 2 in progress: [T008](../../board/TASKS.md#t008 "engine-smoke script and the first ledger row"), [T015](../../board/TASKS.md#t015 "Re-run engine-smoke on each engine once one is present"), [T019](../../board/TASKS.md#t019 "Own Stomp engine-smoke row once the human approves running the local…"), [T024](../../board/TASKS.md#t024 "Synthetic recovery set v1 and its thresholds"), [T040](../../board/TASKS.md#t040 "Parity null (human request)"), [T060](../../board/TASKS.md#t060 "Parity spot checks: at least 5 models per block category on Stomp"), [T066](../../board/TASKS.md#t066 "Parity metrics for time-based effects"), [T073](../../board/TASKS.md#t073 "Gate 2 synthetic recovery"), [T096](../../board/TASKS.md#t096 "Gate 2 option A + rule B"), [T100](../../board/TASKS.md#t100 "Ruling 2 in scripts/synthetic_v1.py"), [T103](../../board/TASKS.md#t103 "Native verification suite scripts/native_verify.py"), [T104](../../board/TASKS.md#t104 "Song and artist context as a prior"), [T107](../../board/TASKS.md#t107 "Blind A/B listening tool scripts/ab_session.py"), [T115](../../board/TASKS.md#t115 "Local Gate 2 report for a non-technical reader"), [T223](../../board/TASKS.md#t223 "Blind validation of AFx-Rep against separated stems")

**Challenges and findings**

- The plan's 'every change' synthetic run can only be a 6-item Native smoke recovery (about 7.5 min); the full Native pass is nightly (about 9.4 h) and Stomp recovery is milestone-only because hardware renders are serial. _[[docs/notes/synthetic-benchmark-design.md](../../docs/notes/synthetic-benchmark-design.md "Synthetic recovery set v1: design, cost, capture request, proposed thresholds (T024)")]_
- Native can only test which of three fixed structures and bypass switches and knobs, so model accuracy is meaningful only on the Stomp sub-set. _[[docs/notes/synthetic-benchmark-design.md](../../docs/notes/synthetic-benchmark-design.md "Synthetic recovery set v1: design, cost, capture request, proposed thresholds (T024)")]_
- A benchmark re-run of stompd from the scratchpad was blocked by the permission system the first time; a benchmark-owned row exists since 18:09:13. _[[board/GATES.md](../../board/GATES.md "Gates") [Gate 1](../../board/GATES.md#gate-1--both-engines-driven-by-code-end-of-week-1 "Gate 1 — both engines driven by code (end of week 1)") note]_
- Cab Early Reflections does not match between the Stomp (firmware 3.50) and Native 3.82: at 0 the search winner nulls at -31.4 dB, at its chosen 0.783 only -3.6 dB, worsening smoothly with the value. Every other parameter at its extreme stayed in parity. Cause untested. The hardware-parity claim holds for Early Reflections at 0 only. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-15 parity finding](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-15-parity-finding-cab-earlyreflections-lead-on-benchmark "2026-10-15 (parity finding: cab EarlyReflections, lead on benchmark)"); ledger [2026-10-15T02:48:51](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L108 "parity · both · parity-v0 · null_residual_db = -31.35 (first of 5 rows at this time)") (5 rows)]_
- Parity rows are -30 to -37 dB on clean and crunch presets and -19 dB on a delay preset; the cause of the delay case is not recorded. A void row (-6.41 dB) came from a stale Native template and is excluded. _[ledger [2026-10-15T00:25:36](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L96 "parity · both · parity-v0 · null_residual_db = -30.96 (first of 9 rows at this time)") (9 rows); [ledger/void.csv](../../ledger/void.csv "ledger/void.csv")]_

#### Learning (predictor, proxies)

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, synthetic

**Purpose.** Learn from rendered pairs to seed the search with a blind predictor and, later, to speed the inner loop with neural block proxies and a learned similarity metric.  
**Done when.** A predictor trained on Helix-rendered pairs improves the search's start (measurably fewer renders to the same loss), its weights have a model card and a licence that allows publishing, and the flywheel adds verified matches as training data.  
**Functionality today.** Heads (logistic regression, small MLP) over AFx-Rep embeddings predict the model and knob positions of a single block; the drive head seeds the predictor-first matcher. Measurement only; nothing admitted.  
**Progress note.** Drive: a predictor trained on 150,000 renders narrows the drive search (85 percent model top-1 on 48 blind Helix targets; PANNs and DAC encoders fell short of AFx-Rep). 2026-10-22/23: slot-dataset-v1 for amp, cab, mod, delay, reverb and EQ rendered (about 240,000 clips) and trained at half and full size; model top-1 on seen players amp 0.54, cab 0.76, delay 0.56, mod 0.75, reverb 0.47, EQ 0.82; on a new player amp 0.19-0.31, cab 0.32-0.35, delay 0.25-0.32, mod 0.47-0.60. Amp silence bug fixed with a refill. The seen-player numbers rest on 18 held-out DI files from two players (the test clips are cut from those files; they are not thousands of independent items). No slot head is in a matcher. 2026-10-23 [T214](../../board/TASKS.md#t214 "Chain transfer test (human 2026-10-23 \"yes\")") chain transfer test (295 finished chains, held-out DIs): the heads do NOT transfer: MLP model top-1 chain vs single block drive 0.02 vs 0.93, amp 0.01 vs 0.54, cab 0.25 vs 0.76, mod 0.21 vs 0.75, delay 0.10 vs 0.56, reverb 0.28 vs 0.47, EQ 0.12 vs 0.82; knobs within 0.15 fall to 0.31-0.43. The 'constant mid-range guess scores about 0.3' baseline quoted against those knob numbers was not re-measured. Status stays prototype: trained, not verified. [T215](../../board/TASKS.md#t215 "Chain training pilot") chain-rendered training pilot (about 5,000 chains) is running on the human's 'yes start pilot'; no bar is registered. 2026-10-26: the product is now drive -&gt; EQ -&gt; reverb (human 2026-10-25), so the amp, cabinet, modulation and delay slot heads are out of scope for now. [T233](../../board/TASKS.md#t233 "Product-chain predictor") (human 'Do both now') is rendering about 40,000 drive -&gt; optional EQ -&gt; optional reverb chains from the two library players plus the human's own session recordings (15 percent of draws), then trains heads for drive model and level-conditioned knobs, reverb presence/type/decay/mix and EQ. Render phase at 2026-10-26 16:23: shard 96 of 105, 37,998 chains ok, 2 dropped, deadline 20:00, then training. No result, no ledger row, nothing adopted.  
**Board tasks.** 7 of 10 done, 2 in progress: [T030](../../board/TASKS.md#t030 "Data generator: EGDB DI clips through random plausible chains on…"), [T195](../../board/TASKS.md#t195 "Permissive encoders PANNs and DAC vs AFx-Rep on a drive-dataset-v1…"), [T202](../../board/TASKS.md#t202 "Training-data coverage report per slot"), [T208](../../board/TASKS.md#t208 "Commands for the next training sessions on the other slots"), [T210](../../board/TASKS.md#t210 "slot-dataset-v1 amp: plan + first 2-hour batch"), [T212](../../board/TASKS.md#t212 "Diagnose the slot-dataset-v1 amp \"Native returned silence\" failures…"), [T213](../../board/TASKS.md#t213 "slot-dataset-v1 overnight run"), [T214](../../board/TASKS.md#t214 "Chain transfer test (human 2026-10-23 \"yes\")"), [T215](../../board/TASKS.md#t215 "Chain training pilot"), [T233](../../board/TASKS.md#t233 "Product-chain predictor")

**Challenges and findings**

- The Native EULA is silent on training ML on Native renders; prefer hardware renders for anything published until the installer EULAs are read. _[[docs/notes/line6-eula-flags.md](../../docs/notes/line6-eula-flags.md "Line 6 EULA flags for automation and protocol use (not legal advice)"); PLAN risk table]_
- 240,000 single-block renders were made before the one-hour chain check; [T202](../../board/TASKS.md#t202 "Training-data coverage report per slot") had already named 'no chains' as the gap. [T214](../../board/TASKS.md#t214 "Chain transfer test (human 2026-10-23 \"yes\")") then showed the heads at or near chance inside chains, because a block in front hurts and a cab after nearly erases. _[[docs/notes/methodology-audit-2026-10-23.md](../../docs/notes/methodology-audit-2026-10-23.md "Methodology audit, 2026-10-23 (independent, read-only)") section 0; [docs/notes/chain-transfer-t214.md](../../docs/notes/chain-transfer-t214.md "chain-transfer-v1: do the single-block heads still name their block inside a chain? #### Blind listening sessions and export

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** ear (open listening, one negative blind session)

**Purpose.** Prepare level-matched, randomised blind A/B or MUSHRA sessions so the human, who is the final judge, can rate matches against references and baselines.  
**Done when.** A blind-ab-session script builds a session folder with the answer key kept out, the human has rated the 20-song set, results are ingested as ledger rows, and [Gate 5](../../board/GATES.md#gate-5--beats-the-nearest-preset-on-real-songs-was-gate-3-then-gate-4-renumbered-2026-10-17-and-2026-10-18 "Gate 5 — beats the nearest preset on real songs (was Gate 3, then Gate 4; renumbered 2026-10-17 and 2026-10-18)") has the human's sign-off.  
**Functionality today.** No blind session can be prepared yet. Informal listening happened outside the tool: the human compared the target stem, two matches, the start chain and the raw DI for one section of one song, and played one preset live on the Stomp. Update: [scripts/ab_session.py](../../scripts/ab_session.py "Blind A/B listening sessions (Gate 2 and Gate 3 optional, Gate 4 pedal listening and Gate 5 real songs require") (build, score, gate2-demo) now exists with 7 tests; no session has been prepared or rated.  
**Progress note.** Seven short blind rating sessions (v1 to v7, 2026-10-18 to 2026-10-20; 6 to 28 trials each) were built and rated for drive matching and one chain tuning; none covered the seven song matches, whose listening was open. The human's test-retest consistency on repeated blind pairs is 7 of 12 across sessions, so the sessions are directional. The lead's kind=ab rows that transcribe the human's informal verdicts (2026-10-20T19:17, 2026-10-22T18:40) are not session ratings and are not aggregated as ab evidence. The 20-song set does not exist.  
**Board tasks.** 1 of 1 done: [T107](../../board/TASKS.md#t107 "Blind A/B listening tool scripts/ab_session.py")  
**Owners.** benchmark

#### End-to-end song-to-preset pipeline and CLI

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, real-songs, ear (open listening, one negative blind session)

**Purpose.** Give it a song, get back an HX Stomp .hlx with up to three snapshots, verified on the pedal.  
**Done when.** One command takes a song (and optional stems), runs separation, segmentation, DI choice, search and export, and returns a validated .hlx whose result is verified on the engine of the user's choice.  
**Functionality today.** [scripts/match_song.py](../../scripts/match_song.py "End-to-end match of ONE song section on Helix Native (T041). LOCAL ONLY: no song audio leaves the machine.") takes one prepared song section to an exported .hlx and a listening pack on one machine; the human then loaded the preset on the pedal and listened. Not a product command: one song (DS-Sample), unadmitted loss, hand-run steps, and nothing for the other sections.  
**Progress note.** A script runs the chain on a real song locally (separation cache, section choice, DI pick, search on Native, .hlx export); one song has been taken through it and the result was loaded onto the pedal by hand with the whole-preset injection tool. There is still no CLI entry point, no pipeline module, no automatic Stomp verification step, no snapshot assignment from section groups and only one song. 2026-10-22/23: seven songs run through hand-run scripts (separation, rebuilt dry guitar, full drive search, knob fine-tune, level-matched listening pages and presets, one per song); drive only, no chain, no automatic pedal check. 2026-10-26: scope narrowed by the human to drive -&gt; EQ -&gt; reverb; delay on hold; amp and cabinet only as a possible stand-in for the drive. Still hand-run; the first blind check of the drive-only matches was negative.  
**Board tasks.** 5 of 5 done: [T041](../../board/TASKS.md#t041 "End-to-end on a real song"), [T200](../../board/TASKS.md#t200 "Blind single-drive matches of five songs the human added to data/songs"), [T207](../../board/TASKS.md#t207 "All seven songs (human 2026-10-22 \"render the dry tracks for all of…"), [T209](../../board/TASKS.md#t209 "Knob fine-tuning on the seven T207 picks"), [T211](../../board/TASKS.md#t211 "All seven songs with the calibrated guitar")

**Challenges and findings**

- The first end-to-end run found four integration problems at once: a level-blind loss, an ignored exact 0.0 command, host memory of 5 to 11 GB with two kills, and a strong DI dependence of the loss. The first two memory and 0.0 findings are fixed in the engine; the loss problems are not. _[board T041; [DECISIONS 2026-10-14 search T041](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-search-t041-first-end-to-end-run-on-ds-sample-proposals-for-the-lead "2026-10-14 (search, T041 first end-to-end run on DS-Sample; proposals for the lead)"), 2026-10-15 host memory]_
- The song audio and stems are local only (gitignored cache); this page and the repository carry no audio. The song is referred to by its file id DS-Sample only. _[CLAUDE.md standing rules]_

**Still to do**

- Define the CLI and pipeline module
- Wire hardware verification of the top candidate and the play-along capture
- Snapshot assignment from section groups (up to three snapshots)
- Run more songs (the 20-song set)

**Open blockers**

- Depends on the [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") verification and sign-off, the chain builder (not built), a score that works without a clean recording, and automatic hardware verification.

**Decisions**

- Fixed 8-slot template for v0 ([DECISIONS 2026-10-13 planning](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-13-planning-before-the-repo "2026-10-13 (planning, before the repo)")), since superseded by real @position order in the preset code

**Risks**

- Several guitars in one stem: medium
- Studio processing on the record: medium

#### Platform, install and packaging

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests

**Purpose.** Install and run on Windows, macOS and Linux with whichever GPU backend is present, and ship releases others can use.  
**Done when.** A clean install works on all three OSes in CI, GPU backends are packaged, Native on Linux works through Wine and yabridge or is documented as unsupported, and a release package exists.  
**Functionality today.** pip install -e .\[dev\] works on this Mac; detect_backend picks cpu or mps; CI runs ruff and pytest on Ubuntu with Python 3.11 only.  
**Progress note.** Dev-environment notes, a GPU backend detector and a Linux-only CI for tests and lint exist; the backend is verified only as CPU and MPS on one arm64 Mac. No Windows or Linux run, no packaging, and the pyproject has no entry points. The project licence is now MIT. 2026-10-17: elastic job runner ([T138](../../board/TASKS.md#t138 "Elastic jobs for the host scaler")) and Native host cap raised to 8.

#### Licensing and legal hygiene

**Status:** working · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests

**Purpose.** Keep Line 6 assets out of the repo, respect the GPLv3 boundary and dependency licences, and flag EULA and model-weight questions before they bite.  
**Done when.** licence-check passes on every dependency change with every WARN justified, the EULAs have been read, the project licence is chosen, and no release ships a non-commercial or unlicensed component.  
**Functionality today.** licence_check.py scans dependency licences, the pedalboard import boundary, Line 6 assets and committed golden presets and writes a review; it passes today. The project is MIT; third-party components keep their own licences.  
**Progress note.** licence_check.py runs and passes with documented WARN rulings, the pedalboard boundary is enforced by a test, no Line 6 asset is in the repo, and the project licence is MIT (LICENSE present, THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES updated; the copyright line is derived from the GitHub name and awaits the human's confirmation). Not done: the human marked the installer EULAs as read but recorded no finding, diffq is non-commercial (private research only), the guitar-capable RoFormer checkpoint has no stated licence, and the WARN dependencies need re-review before any bundled release.  
**Board tasks.** 6 of 6 done: [T009](../../board/TASKS.md#t009 "Pull the Native Pilot's Guide automation details and fretwire's…"), [T011](../../board/TASKS.md#t011 "First licence-check run over pyproject.toml once the engine spikes…"), [T016](../../board/TASKS.md#t016 "Fix the DeprecationWarning in scripts/licence_check.py"), [T016a](../../board/TASKS.md#t016a "Remaining from T016: DeprecationWarning at scripts/licence_check.py:47"), [T017](../../board/TASKS.md#t017 "Read the EULA shipped with the Native and HX Edit installers"), [T020b](../../board/TASKS.md#t020b "RoFormer adapter licence read + install")  
**Owners.** reviewer, research

**Implemented**

- licence_check.py, allowlist with DECISIONS references, review reports [`scripts/licence_check.py`](../../scripts/licence_check.py "licence-check (owner: reviewer). See .claude/skills/licence-check/SKILL.md.") [`docs/licence-allowlist.json`](../../docs/licence-allowlist.json "docs/licence-allowlist.json") [`reviews/licence-2026-10-13.md`](../../reviews/licence-2026-10-13.md "licence-check 2026-10-13") [`reviews/licence-2026-10-14.md`](../../reviews/licence-2026-10-14.md "licence-check 2026-10-14")

A WARN is not a clearance: soxr, lameenc, tqdm, certifi need re-review before any bundled release. _[[DECISIONS 2026-10-14 RoFormer rulings](../../docs/DECISIONS.md#2026-10-14-cycle-3-roformer-extra-licence-rulings-lead "2026-10-14 (cycle 3, RoFormer extra licence rulings, lead)"); board [T016a](../../board/TASKS.md#t016a "Remaining from T016: DeprecationWarning at scripts/licence_check.py:47")]_

- No Line 6 data or binaries in the repo (same entry)

#### Block-presence detector

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, synthetic

**Purpose.** Estimate which kinds of block (cabinet, gain stage, delay, reverb, modulation, compressor, equaliser) a target tone contains, to seed the chain builder.  
**Done when.** Passes the registered per-category bars on a held-out split and works behind an amp and cabinet.  
**Functionality today.** Returns per-category probabilities from a target and its clean recording (known-DI mode) or without it (no-DI mode).  
**Progress note.** Registered before scoring (presence-v1), scored once on the test split: only the cabinet (0.89 precision, 0.95 recall) and the gain-index ordering (0.77) pass; drive-or-amp, delay, reverb, modulation, compressor and equaliser miss their bars. Without the clean recording only "is there a gain stage" is usable. Accepted as a soft prior only.  
**Board tasks.** 1 of 1 done: [T102](../../board/TASKS.md#t102 "Block-presence detector for the chain builder")  
**Owners.** none

**Implemented**

- Presence features, model and evaluation script [`src/tonematch/metrics/presence.py`](../../src/tonematch/metrics/presence.py "Block-presence detector (T102): which effect categories a target guitar recording contains.") [`scripts/presence_eval.py`](../../scripts/presence_eval.py "Block-presence evaluation `presence-v1` (owner: tone-metrics, T102). Offline: no Native, no pedal.") [`tests/test_presence.py`](../../tests/test_presence.py "Block-presence detector (T102): measurements on synthetic signals, and invariance to the notes played.")

**Evidence**

- **unit-tests**: [tests/test_presence.py](../../tests/test_presence.py "Block-presence detector (T102): measurements on synthetic signals, and invariance to the notes played.") (14)
- **synthetic**: ledger [2026-10-17T17:21:45+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L776 "benchmark · native · presence-v1 · macro_f1_known_di = 0.6616 (first of 10 rows at this time)") (10 rows) presence-v1 test split: macro F1 0.66 known-DI; cab and gain index pass; ledger [2026-10-17T17:25:14+00:00](../../ledger/runs.csv?plain=1#L804 "benchmark · both · presence-v1 · chain_extra_top1_known_di = 0.152 (first of 2 rows at this time)") (2 rows) chains diagnostic: single-block models call an extra block behind an amp and cabinet a drive

#### Chain builder

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests

**Purpose.** Turn the analysis (blocks present, common patterns, per-slot model shortlists) into a whole chain and a preset file.  
**Done when.** Runs on a real engine and beats the nearest community preset on real songs ([Gate 5](../../board/GATES.md#gate-5--beats-the-nearest-preset-on-real-songs-was-gate-3-then-gate-4-renumbered-2026-10-17-and-2026-10-18 "Gate 5 — beats the nearest preset on real songs (was Gate 3, then Gate 4; renumbered 2026-10-17 and 2026-10-18)")).  
**Functionality today.** Assembles candidate chains using block-serial composition of single-block templates.  
**Progress note.** Built ([T105](../../board/TASKS.md#t105 "Chain builder (human 2026-10-17 \"build a chain from the analysis\")")) with fake engines only: presence, top structures from the chain prior, per-slot shortlist, coordinate descent and joint refine, halving across structures. A real run needs a block-library adapter, block-serial rendering in the render pool and the presence estimate keys.  
**Board tasks.** 3 of 3 done: [T105](../../board/TASKS.md#t105 "Chain builder (human 2026-10-17 \"build a chain from the analysis\")"), [T101](../../board/TASKS.md#t101 "Chain-pattern model for the chain builder"), [T109](../../board/TASKS.md#t109 "Curated corpus analysis")

#### Cabinet estimation by deconvolution

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, proxy

**Purpose.** Compute a cabinet response from a target and its clean recording instead of searching for it.  
**Done when.** Picks the matching cabinet on real clips with the right amp, and works with an estimated amp.  
**Functionality today.** Computes a cabinet response in about 30 ms and matches it to the nearest cabinet model or library IR.  
**Progress note.** Analysis only ([T106](../../board/TASKS.md#t106 "IR library analysis (human 2026-10-17)")): with the right amp the cabinet model ranks first in 22 of 24 real-clip cases and an IR is recovered exactly 94 percent of the time; with a wrong amp model it fails (2 percent). IR audio and per-file data stay local; the library may be used for training (human, 2026-10-17).  
**Board tasks.** 1 of 1 done: [T106](../../board/TASKS.md#t106 "IR library analysis (human 2026-10-17)")  
**Owners.** none

**Implemented**

- Cabinet response, IR library analysis [`src/tonematch/metrics/cab_response.py`](../../src/tonematch/metrics/cab_response.py "Cab and impulse-response (IR) frequency responses: measure, describe, compare, estimate.") #### Per-family specialists

**Status:** working · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** synthetic

**Purpose.** One method per effect family (gain, linear, dynamics, time-based, modulation) with its own measurements, search and budget share.  
**Done when.** Each specialist beats the general method on its family at the same budget, judged by a paired comparison, and [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") passes on a fresh set.  
**Functionality today.** --specialists all routes each family to its specialist and refiner.  
**Progress note.** Five families have a specialist plugged in through a dispatcher ([T117](../../board/TASKS.md#t117 "Linear specialist for cab and EQ") linear, [T122](../../board/TASKS.md#t122 "Family dispatch and time-based specialist") time-based, mod, amp, gain; [T134](../../board/TASKS.md#t134 "Specialist adapters (blocking --specialists all for Gate 2 attempt 2)") and [T140](../../board/TASKS.md#t140 "Linear specialist wired into --specialists all\|linear") adapters). Each was adopted only after beating the general method on development items. In [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 2 on sealed synthetic-v2 the combined method raised category to 0.865 and model to 0.760, but modulation (model 4 of 12) and drive (6 of 12) remain weak and the gate failed on ranking the true preset first (0.9375). Dynamics is a stub. Synthetic evidence only; no ear.  
**Board tasks.** 4 of 4 done: [T117](../../board/TASKS.md#t117 "Linear specialist for cab and EQ"), [T122](../../board/TASKS.md#t122 "Family dispatch and time-based specialist"), [T134](../../board/TASKS.md#t134 "Specialist adapters (blocking --specialists all for Gate 2 attempt 2)"), [T140](../../board/TASKS.md#t140 "Linear specialist wired into --specialists all\|linear")  
**Owners.** search, tone-metrics

#### Colour stage after the drive: EQ and reverb

**Status:** prototype · **Health:** partial · **Evidence:** unit-tests, synthetic, real-songs, ear (open listening, one negative blind session)

**Purpose.** Lightly colour the drive match with an equaliser and a reverb chosen for the song, as the human's refocus defines the product chain (drive -&gt; EQ -&gt; reverb).  
**Done when.** Given a song's separated guitar and its drive match, the tool names an equaliser setting and a reverb (type, decay, mix) that blind listeners hear as closer than the drive match alone, on songs it was not tuned on, with a bar the human approved first.  
**Functionality today.** A bounded equaliser fit from the long-term spectrum ratio, a reverb candidate grid, listening packs and comparison pages, and a blind reverb estimator (decay and mix from a stem's note tails). None of it is wired into the song-match command.  
**Progress note.** 2026-10-25: colour pilots on three then seven songs ([T228](../../board/TASKS.md#t228 "Colour pilot (human 2026-10-25 \"focus on our distortion model… drive…"), [T229](../../board/TASKS.md#t229 "Colour comparison on seven songs")). Open listening: a light reverb after the drive was preferred on all seven songs, the fitted equaliser variants could not be told apart (kept, human: 'It didn't hurt anything'), an amp in place of the drive was mixed. One blind Mother pack: the fitted-EQ version was closest. Reverb pilot ([T230](../../board/TASKS.md#t230 "Reverb-model pilot (human 2026-10-25 \"yes\")"), 27 candidates per song, pre-registered, blind ranks on six songs, Cherub Rock excluded): no computable signal agreed with the human (AFx-Rep rho 0.05, note-tail 0.04, time-specialist -0.09, dip fill -0.40); the human's repeats were consistent (mean rank difference 0.33 against chance 2.67); Plate was not preferred blind (mean rank by type: Room 3.9, Hall 4.0, Plate 4.4, ties frequent). Blind reverb estimator ([T231](../../board/TASKS.md#t231 "Blind reverb estimator")): validated on 189 known reverbs (decay ordering 36 of 40 triples, rho 0.85-0.89 on 5 songs; mix rho 0.88-0.94; type not separable, 43 percent against 33 chance); on the full stems a real tail is readable on 2 of 7 (Mother 0.70 s, Territorial 0.90 s), the other 5 read the separator's residue. Confirmation ([T232](../../board/TASKS.md#t232 "Pre-registered confirmation of the T231 reverb estimator on Mother…"), pre-registered): Mother confirmed (named closest ranked 1 of 7, named furthest 7 of 7), Territorial not reproducible (the human's repeat got ranks 4 and 1), pooled not confirmed. No reverb model exists; a fixed default was rejected by the human.  
**Board tasks.** 3 of 5 done, 2 in progress: [T228](../../board/TASKS.md#t228 "Colour pilot (human 2026-10-25 \"focus on our distortion model… drive…"), [T229](../../board/TASKS.md#t229 "Colour comparison on seven songs"), [T230](../../board/TASKS.md#t230 "Reverb-model pilot (human 2026-10-25 \"yes\")"), [T231](../../board/TASKS.md#t231 "Blind reverb estimator"), [T232](../../board/TASKS.md#t232 "Pre-registered confirmation of the T231 reverb estimator on Mother…")  
**Owners.** search, tone-metrics

Blind reverb ranks on six songs ([T230](../../board/TASKS.md#t230 "Reverb-model pilot (human 2026-10-25 \"yes\")")) and a confirmation on two ([T232](../../board/TASKS.md#t232 "Pre-registered confirmation of the T231 reverb estimator on Mother…")), see progress note

**Challenges and findings**

- Direct measurement found no delay or reverb on any song in the stems and could not have seen a light one: the rebuilt dry is not the real performance ([T228](../../board/TASKS.md#t228 "Colour pilot (human 2026-10-25 \"focus on our distortion model… drive…")). _[[docs/notes/colour-pilot-t228.md](../../docs/notes/colour-pilot-t228.md "Colour pilot, T228 (T228)")]_
- On five of seven stems the 'tail' is the separator's residue (-10 to -13 dB floor, same curve as the full mix); the human said not to work on the separator (2026-10-25). _[[docs/notes/reverb-estimator-t231.md](../../docs/notes/reverb-estimator-t231.md "Blind reverb estimator, T231 (T231)")]_
- Reverb type (Room, Plate, Hall) is not separable by the estimator, and the human's blind ranks did not prefer Plate. _[[docs/notes/reverb-pilot-t230.md](../../docs/notes/reverb-pilot-t230.md "Reverb-model pilot, T230\") (T230)")]_

**Still to do**

- Rule on the reverb model: estimate decay and mix when the tail is readable, fall back otherwise (human)
- Redo Cherub Rock on another section
- Test the estimator on more songs with a pre-registered named pick
- Compare the [T233](../../board/TASKS.md#t233 "Product-chain predictor") reverb heads with the estimator on the seven songs

**Open blockers**

- No reverb model: nothing computed from the stems agreed with the human's reverb ranks, the estimator reads a real tail on only 2 of 7 stems (the rest is separator residue), and the confirmation held on one song of two. The human rejected a fixed default and asked for a model.

**Decisions**

- Chain is drive -&gt; EQ -&gt; reverb; no amp or cab; delay on hold (human, DECISIONS 2026-10-25)
- EQ options stay in the chain; a fixed reverb default is rejected; a reverb model is required (human, DECISIONS 2026-10-25)
- No separator work; the stems stay as they are (human, DECISIONS 2026-10-25)

**Risks**

- Light reverb was preferred in open listening on all seven songs, but not blind; the preference may be for 'more ambience', not for the song's own reverb.

### Evidence classes of the headline claims

Every headline claim, with the class of evidence behind it, from the independent methodology audit of 2026-10-23, extended on 2026-10-26 with the claims of 25 and 26 October. Only [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") reaches class A; the human has disowned it and it is closed as superseded.

- **Class A**: Blind, on a sealed set, with the pass bar registered before the run.
- **Class B**: Blind, but on an unsealed set (built by the agent that built the method).
- **Class C**: An open listen by the human, who knew which candidate was which.
- **Class D**: Numbers only: an unadmitted measure, or no bar, or a set drawn after the method was built.
- **Class E**: No evidence recorded.

| Claim | Class | n | Limits | Evidence |

The human judged the model 'garbage'; the confirmation run and harness checks are open. | [Gate 2](../../board/GATES.md#gate-2--recovers-known-presets-end-of-week-3 "Gate 2 — recovers known presets (end of week 3)") attempt 3 on all 96 sealed synthetic-v3 items: all 7 checks met (rank-1 0.979 under the extended rule B); closed as superseded by the human on 2026-10-25, not signed; Decision log; Methodology audit, 2026-10-23 (independent, read-only; evidence classes, overstatements found) |

## Glossary

| Term | Meaning |
|---|---|
| AFx-Rep | A learned listening measure (a neural network embedding) used here to order candidate presets by how close they sound to the target. Lower distance is closer. |
| approved-methods file | A list in the repository of method settings the human has ruled on, with the human's words; a test fails if the code's defaults drift from it. |
| Basic Pitch | A free, Apache-2.0 licensed program from Spotify that writes down the notes in a recording. Used here to turn a separated guitar part into notes, which are then played back as a clean guitar. |
| blind A/B | A listening test where the listener does not know which option is which. |
| blind reverb estimator | A calculation that reads a reverb's decay and amount from the tails left at the end of notes in a recording, without knowing the reverb setting. |
| block | One element in a preset's chain: an amp, cabinet, drive pedal, delay and so on. |
| block library | A stored set of single-block Native setups, one per pedal effect model, used to build and render candidate presets quickly. |
| bootstrap interval | A range for a measured difference, built by re-sampling the 96 items many times. If the whole range sits on the good side of a line, the difference is reliable at the stated confidence. |
| capture | The one-off step that teaches Helix Native a new chain: the preset file is loaded into its editor and the resulting state is recorded. |
| chain builder | The planned stage that decides which kinds of block a tone needs, orders them by common patterns learned from real presets, and then fills each slot using the single-block methods. |
| class C / class B | Evidence classes from the audit: C is an open listen (the listener knew which was which); B is blind on an unsealed set. See 'evidence class'. |
| CMA-ES | An optimisation algorithm used to search for suitable settings. |
| CoreAudio | The Mac's audio system. When it fails, no sound can be sent to or recorded from the pedal. |
| dB | Decibel, a logarithmic loudness or level-difference scale. |
| dBFS | Decibels relative to full scale: how loud a digital signal is. 0 is the loudest possible; -36 is much quieter than -28. |
| DI | Direct Input: a clean, unprocessed guitar recording, before any amp or effects. |
| different dry tracks | Two runs whose clean-guitar input differs (other notes, tone or level). A distance between their renders and the target mixes the effect of the guitar with the effect of the preset, so it cannot say which preset is closer. |
| DSP | Digital Signal Processing: the audio calculations inside amps and effects. |
| Early Reflections | A cabinet setting that adds the first short echoes of the room. |
| effect family | A group of effects that behave alike: gain (drive, fuzz, amp), linear (cabinet, equaliser), dynamics (compressor, gate), time-based (delay, reverb), modulation (chorus, flanger, phaser, tremolo). |
| EQ | Equaliser: a tone control that boosts or cuts parts of the frequency spectrum, such as bass or treble. |
| EULA | The software licence agreement shown when installing a product. |
| evidence class | How strong a claim's evidence is, from the audit: A blind with a sealed set and a bar registered first; B blind on an unsealed set; C an open listen by the human (knowing which is which); D numbers only, on an unadmitted measure or without a bar; E no evidence. |
| fine-tuning (knobs) | After the block model is chosen, a local search that adjusts only its knobs to bring the render closer to the target. |
| firmware | The software running inside the pedal. |
| fw | Short for firmware, the pedal's built-in software. The pedal was on version 3.50 until 2026-10-15 and is now on 3.80. |
| gate | A milestone with a written checklist and a human sign-off. |
| golden file | A reference preset file the owner exported from HX Edit or Native, used to check that our own files follow the same layout. |
| guitar profile | A one-off calibration of one guitar: its normal playing level, its tone compared with the library guitars, and a bank of its own notes. |
| held-out | Test items not used when tuning, so the result is not flattered by tuning. |
| Helix Native | Line 6's software version of the Helix sounds; it runs as a plugin and renders audio offline. |
| Hum and Ripple | Two amp settings that add a mains-hum style noise. The noise runs freely, so it differs between renders of the same preset. |
| HX Edit | Line 6's editing program for the pedal; it also imports preset files. |
| HX Stomp | The Line 6 guitar-effects pedal this project targets. |
| impulse response (IR) | A short recording of a speaker cabinet and microphone that can reproduce that cabinet's sound by convolution. A cabinet block in the pedal uses one. |
| invariance ratio | How much a listening measure moves with a change of clean guitar compared with a knob move. Above 1 means the guitar matters more than the knob; the learned measure scored 30 for small knob moves. |
| known-answer set | A test set made by rendering presets we already know through Helix, so we can check whether the tool recovers them. |
| ledger | The project's append-only record of measured runs; a claim without a row did not happen. |
| look-alike | A candidate preset that differs slightly from the true one (a nearby setting or a neighbouring model); used to test whether a measure can tell near matches apart. |
| loss | The score the optimiser tries to lower: how different two sounds are. Lower is a closer match. |
| LUFS | A loudness measure that follows how loud something sounds. Listening comparisons are level-matched with it so louder does not win. |
| middle baseline | A baseline that sets every knob to the middle of its range and picks nothing cleverly. It shows what a do-nothing guess scores. |
| MIDI CC | A control message that moves one knob or switch from software. |
| new-player generalisation | How well a trained predictor works on a guitarist whose playing it never saw in training. The honest test for a tool that will be used by someone else. |
| null residual | What is left when one recording is subtracted from another that should match. Reported in decibels below the signal; the more negative, the closer. |
| numeric mode | Helix Native starts each session in one of two slightly different rounding modes. The difference is tiny (rounding level) but can break exact cancellation tests. |
| open listen | A listening session where the listener knows which candidate is which. It can flatter a method; a blind session cannot. |
| P3 | The third library guitarist's clean recordings, held back from every training set and every search so a sealed test can use them. |
| parity | Agreement between the two engines: the same preset is rendered on both and the recordings are compared. |
| Path A and Path B | The two signal paths of the HX Stomp. Using both at once renders two chains in one pass. |
| pre-registered | The analysis and the reading of its result were written down, with a hash, before any answer was seen, so the result cannot be bent to fit. |
| pre-roll | Extra audio played before the part that is recorded, so delays and reverbs start from the same state every time. |
| predictor | A trained program that looks at a sound and guesses which block model and settings made it. Used to narrow the search. |
| preset | A saved sound: a chain of amp, cabinet and effect blocks with their settings (an .hlx file). |
| product chain | The chain the product builds, as redefined by the human on 2026-10-25: a drive effect, then an equaliser, then a reverb. No amp, cabinet or delay for now. |
| provisional loss | The early combination of three tone measures used before any measure was admitted. Never admitted; at chance on hard pairs; still the default re-score of the block matcher. |
| random walk | A baseline search that tries 300 random settings and keeps the best, judged by the same trusted score. It scores high here because the tool has only 300 tries over a pool of 47 effect models and the score is a good judge, so even random guessing often lands on the right model. Earlier notes called it random search. |
| rank-1 rate | How often the true preset is ranked first among the candidates (the look-alikes). Higher is better; guessing among 20 gives 0.05. |
| readback | Reading the pedal's settings back after writing them to confirm they match. |
| rebuilt dry track | A stand-in clean guitar made for a song that has none: the notes found in the separated guitar are replayed using real clean guitar notes (from a library or from the player's own calibration session). |
| render | Play a test signal through a preset and record the audio that comes out. |
| render pool | Several copies of Helix Native running at once, each rendering a different candidate, to finish a batch sooner. |
| reverb | An effect that adds the sound of a space (room, plate, hall) after the guitar. Decay is how long the tail lasts; Mix is how much of it is blended in. |
| sealed set | A test set generated after the bars are registered, with hashes recorded, that no agent reads before the one scoring run. |
| search test | One candidate setting tried by the search: it is rendered and then scored against the target. |
| sentinel | A check run inside a long job that renders a fixed test now and then and stops the job if the result has drifted, so a corrupted render cannot be scored as real. |
| separation | Splitting a full mix into instruments so the guitar can be analysed alone. |
| separator leftovers | Sound from the band (bass, drums, vocals) that the guitar separator fails to remove. It can look like a reverb tail and hide a real one. |
| session close | The last message a control program sends to the pedal when it is done. Without it the pedal stays in editing mode and its blocks cannot be edited by hand. |
| slot | A fixed position in the signal chain: drive, amp, cabinet, modulation, delay, reverb, equaliser, in that order. Each slot is optional. |
| snapshot | One of up to three stored variations of a preset that the pedal can switch between. |
| specialist | A separate method for one family of effects (gain, linear, dynamics, time-based, modulation) with its own measurements and search, instead of one general method for everything. |
| stand-in signals | Test audio made by ordinary software effects instead of Helix. Useful for early checks; not proof the method works on Helix. |
| synthetic-v2 | A fresh known-answer set, made with the same generator and the same frozen rules but a new random seed, because the first set has now been seen. |
| test-retest consistency | How often the listener gives the same answer when a blind pair is repeated. The human's is 7 of 12 across the sessions so far, so small sessions cannot resolve small differences. |
| time-variant block | An effect that changes its sound on its own over time (for example a free-running chorus), so two renders never cancel exactly. |
| tone comparison | The loss: the combined measures of how similar two guitar sounds are. |
| top-1 / top-3 | How often the correct answer is the first choice, or among the first three choices. |
| trust check | A test of whether a measure puts the true preset first among many candidates. |
| USB slip | A one-sample shift or short burst in the audio coming back from the pedal over its USB connection, which makes a render differ slightly from an identical earlier one. |
| wall-clock | Real elapsed time on a clock, as opposed to computing time. |
